        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Sprint Plan Document for Thread/Variant/Iteration-Video Layer

## Overview
Produce `docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`: a 2-week (10 working day) sprint plan that turns `docs/design-thread-layer.md`'s Settled Decisions into day-by-day deliverables. The deliverable is a planning document, not code. Every Settled Decision (SD-001…SD-040) is a hard constraint; SD-035 fixes the week split, SD-036 fixes scope cuts. The document must include: one-page exec summary, day-by-day plan with per-day acceptance criteria, critical path, risks + mitigations, integration test plan (rolling up SD-037), dogfood plan against `runs/artagents_logo_v3`, and explicit resolutions to the six Open Questions in the design doc.

Repo grounding (verified):
- Chokepoint exists: `artagents/executors/runner.py:75` (`run_executor`), and `artagents/orchestrators/runner.py`.
- Variant-producing tools exist: `artagents/executors/generate_image/run.py`, `artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py`.
- Fixture exists: `runs/artagents_logo_v3/` with `concepts.json`, `grid.jpg`, `logo-manifest.json`, `logo-plan.json`, `prompts.json`, `images/logo-00N.png`.
- `tests/` is pytest-based with substantial existing coverage that must remain green (SD-034, SD-037).
- `SKILL.md` exists at repo root (touchpoint for SD-038).

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Author the exec summary (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
Write `# Sprint Plan: Thread / Variant / Iteration-Video Layer` followed by a one-page `## Executive Summary` (~40 lines): goal, why-now (SD-008 irreversibility), scope-in (SD-035), scope-out (SD-036 verbatim), critical path one-liner, top 3 risks with one-line mitigations, definition-of-done.

### Step 2: Author Hard Constraints recap (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
Add `## Hard Constraints (from design)` mapping each load-bearing SD to the day it lands. Include the fail-closed reminder: default `role: ancillary`; only `generate_image` and `logo_ideas` opt in to `variant` per SD-031.

### Step 3: Author Open Question resolutions (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
Add `## Open Question Resolutions`. Resolve all 6 OQs:
- OQ-1 day allocation → see Week 1/2 sections below.
- OQ-2 summarize parallelism → `concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4)` (I/O-bound).
- OQ-3 prefix line format → width `min(120, $COLUMNS)`, ANSI color iff TTY and not `$NO_COLOR`, label truncated to 32 chars with `…`, never truncate IDs.
- OQ-4 reaper timing → lazy on first index read per process.
- OQ-5 docs path → `docs/threads.md`.
- OQ-6 quality_floor formula → `0.5*fraction_with_parent_run_ids + 0.3*fraction_with_brief_content_sha256 + 0.2*fraction_with_input_artifact_resolved`, default floor 0.6 per SD-024.

### Step 4: Author Week 1 — Data Layer days 1-5 (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
Add `## Week 1: Data Layer (Days 1–5)` with `### Day N` subsections (Goal / Deliverables / SDs landed / Acceptance / Tests):
- Day 1: Index foundation — `artagents/threads/{ids,index,schema}.py`, `fcntl.flock` + atomic write + `.bak` rotate (SD-003), Crockford ULIDs (SD-004), schema_version (SD-039), repo-relative paths (SD-040).
- Day 2: `run.json` + chokepoint wrapper — `artagents/threads/{record,wrapper}.py`, modify `artagents/executors/runner.py:75` and `artagents/orchestrators/runner.py`, all SD-008 fields produced, no-op gates per SD-030, `xxhash` for inputs_digest + sha256 for outputs (SD-032), `host_id` (SD-012), `agent_version`.
- Day 3: Auto-attribute + prefix lines + lifecycle CLI — `artagents/threads/{attribute,prefix}.py`, 5-branch decision (SD-006) including lineage inference, prefix per SD-018, `thread {new,list,show,archive,reopen}` (SD-007), reaper (SD-033) lazy.
- Day 4: Variants primitive + selection — extend `output_artifact` (SD-013, SD-026), `selections.jsonl` (SD-014), `groups.json` (SD-016), `chosen_from_groups` edge (SD-015), 5 gestures only per SD-017, `[variants]` nag, executor patches per SD-031.
- Day 5: Brief snapshot + provenance + Week-1 polish — `brief.copy.txt` + `brief_content_sha256` (SD-009), provenance block in `hype.metadata.json` (SD-019), `docs/threads.md` + SKILL.md paragraph + inspect footer (SD-038), `thread backfill` (SD-034).

### Step 5: Author Week 2 — Iteration Video MVP days 6-10 (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
Add `## Week 2: Iteration Video MVP (Days 6–10)`:
- Day 6: Modality registry + `iteration.collect` — `artagents/modalities/{image_grid,audio_waveform,generic_card}.py` (SD-025), `python3 -m artagents modalities {list,inspect}`, `artagents/executors/iteration_collect/` provenance-graph walk (SD-023), data_quality compute (OQ-6).
- Day 7: `iteration.summarize` + `iteration.score` — parallel `builtin.understand` via ThreadPoolExecutor max_workers=4 (OQ-2), brief-diff captions, deterministic ordering.
- Day 8: `iteration.assemble` + quality floor — `(kind,preview_modes)` → renderer (SD-026), precedence `theme>direction>style>defaults` (SD-027), audio-bed selection (SD-028, never generative), quality floor refusal with backfill commands (SD-024), `--force` logged in provenance.
- Day 9: `builtin.iteration_video` orchestrator + report + loud fallback — chain SD-021, 5-output variant group SD-022, `inspect` subcommand + 5 flags (SD-027), `generic_card` loud-fallback annotation + stdout warning (SD-029).
- Day 10: Dogfood + integration suite + buffer — render `runs/artagents_logo_v3`, all 5 outputs, SD-037 suite green, full pytest green.

### Step 6: Author Critical Path + Risks (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
Add `## Critical Path` (Day-1 atomic write → Day-2 wrapper → Day-3 auto-attribute → Day-4 variants → Day-5 brief+provenance → Day-6 collect → Day-8 assemble → Day-9 orchestrator → Day-10 dogfood; Day 5 is the latest possible date for SD-008 metadata). Add `## Risks and Mitigations` covering: lock contention, chokepoint regression, fixture insufficiency, schema bug post-Day-2 (SD-039 migration path), `builtin.understand` rate limits, scope-creep into SD-036.

### Step 7: Author Integration Test Plan + Dogfood Plan (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
Add `## Integration Test Plan` mapping SD-037 line items to concrete test file paths (`tests/test_threads_index.py`, `tests/test_threads_attribute.py`, `tests/test_threads_concurrent.py`, `tests/test_threads_variants.py`, `tests/test_threads_provenance.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video.py`) plus a daily-regression gate (full pytest each day). Add `## Dogfood Plan: runs/artagents_logo_v3` with day-tagged steps: Day 5 backfill (verify roles on logo-00N.png variants vs grid.jpg ancillary), Day 9 inspect, Day 10 render with concrete expected outputs and a manual checklist.

### Step 8: Author closing sections (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
Add `## Out of Scope` (SD-036 verbatim), `## Documentation Deliverables` (mapping SD-038 to docs/threads.md, SKILL.md paragraph, inspect footer — all Day 5), and `## Definition of Done` (SD-008 fields produced for every run after Day 2; SD-037 green; full pytest green; dogfood produces all 5 SD-022 outputs; docs updated; zero modifications to executor `run.py` outside SD-031).

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1-2 (exec summary + constraints) first.
2. Step 3 (OQ resolutions) before day-by-day, since OQs feed concrete day content.
3. Step 4 (Week 1) before Step 5 (Week 2) — Week 2 depends on Week 1.
4. Step 6 (critical path + risks) after both weeks scoped.
5. Step 7 (integration + dogfood) — aggregates concrete file paths from Steps 4-5.
6. Step 8 (closing) last.

## Validation Order
1. Confirm every load-bearing SD appears in some day's "SDs landed" line or in scope/cuts.
2. Confirm all 6 OQs are explicitly resolved.
3. Confirm exec summary fits ~one page.
4. Confirm dogfood names exact files in `runs/artagents_logo_v3/`.
5. Confirm only `docs/sprint-thread-layer.md` is authored.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Sprint Plan Document for Thread/Variant/Iteration-Video Layer

## Overview
Produce `docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`: a 2-week (10 working day) sprint plan that turns `docs/design-thread-layer.md`'s Settled Decisions (now SD-001…SD-044) into day-by-day deliverables. The deliverable is a planning document, not code. SD-035 fixes the week split, SD-036 fixes scope cuts. The newly added SD-041 (privacy/redaction), SD-042 (concurrent variant-selection docs), and SD-043 (iteration-video cost guardrail) are load-bearing and must each land on a specific day with acceptance criteria. SD-044 is non-load-bearing context.

The document must include: one-page exec summary, day-by-day plan with per-day acceptance criteria, critical path, risks + mitigations, integration test plan (rolling up SD-037), dogfood plan against `runs/artagents_logo_v3`, and explicit resolutions to the six Open Questions.

Repo grounding (verified):
- Chokepoint exists: `artagents/executors/runner.py:75` (`run_executor`), and `artagents/orchestrators/runner.py`.
- `ExecutorRunRequest` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:43-54`) and `OrchestratorRunRequest` have **no** `thread` field today; `_cmd_run` in `artagents/executors/cli.py:170` and `artagents/orchestrators/cli.py` build the requests from `--out`, `--input`, `--brief`, `--dry-run`, `--python-exec`, `--verbose` only — no `--thread`/`--variants`/`--from`. CLI plumbing is therefore an explicit prerequisite, not implied work.
- Variant-producing tools exist: `artagents/executors/generate_image/run.py`, `artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py`.
- Fixture exists: `runs/artagents_logo_v3/` with `concepts.json`, `grid.jpg`, `logo-manifest.json`, `logo-plan.json`, `prompts.json`, `images/logo-00N.png`.
- `tests/` is pytest-based with substantial existing coverage that must remain green (SD-034, SD-037).
- `SKILL.md` exists at repo root (touchpoint for SD-038 paragraph and SD-042 last-write-wins documentation).

Approach assessment vs. flags: none of the open flags suggest the plan is targeting the wrong code or wrong root cause. All three concerns are concrete additions — schedule SD-041/042/043, fix the OQ-6 formula, and add explicit CLI/dataclass plumbing on Day 2. The shape of the sprint plan stands; this revision adds tasks and corrects the quality formula.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Author the exec summary (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Open** the document with `# Sprint Plan: Thread / Variant / Iteration-Video Layer`.
2. **Write** a one-page `## Executive Summary` (~40 lines) covering: goal, why-now (SD-008 irreversibility), scope-in (SD-035 + SD-041/042/043), scope-out (SD-036 verbatim), critical path one-liner, top 3 risks with one-line mitigations, definition-of-done. Include one sentence each on SD-041 redaction, SD-042 last-write-wins, SD-043 cost guardrail so the exec summary is current.

### Step 2: Author Hard Constraints recap (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Hard Constraints (from design)` mapping each load-bearing SD (SD-001…SD-043 minus SD-044) to the day it lands. Group by week and call out SD-041, SD-042, SD-043 explicitly.
2. **Add** the fail-closed reminder: default `role: ancillary`; only `generate_image` and `logo_ideas` opt in to `variant` per SD-031.

### Step 3: Author Open Question resolutions (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Open Question Resolutions`. Resolve all 6 OQs:
   - **OQ-1 day allocation** → see Week 1/2 sections below.
   - **OQ-2 summarize parallelism** → `concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4)` (I/O-bound; matches existing repo patterns; avoids asyncio rewrite).
   - **OQ-3 prefix line format** → width `min(120, $COLUMNS)`, ANSI color iff `sys.stdout.isatty()` and `$NO_COLOR` unset, label truncated to 32 chars with `…`, never truncate IDs.
   - **OQ-4 reaper timing** → lazy on first index read per process (matches SD-033 wording).
   - **OQ-5 docs path** → `docs/threads.md` (matches SD-038).
   - **OQ-6 quality_floor formula (CORRECTED)** → distinguish valid roots from missing parent capture per SD-005. Formula: `data_quality = 0.5 * parent_capture_score + 0.3 * fraction_with_brief_content_sha256 + 0.2 * fraction_with_at_least_one_input_artifact_resolved`, where `parent_capture_score = (runs_with_parents + valid_roots) / total_runs` and a run is a `valid_root` iff `input_artifacts == []`. A run with non-empty `input_artifacts` but empty `parent_run_ids` (= unresolved producer hash) is the **only** signal that counts against parent capture. Default floor 0.6 per SD-024. Refusal report names the specific runs whose `input_artifacts` did not resolve to a producer.

### Step 4: Author Week 1 — Data Layer days 1–5 (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** `## Week 1: Data Layer (Days 1–5)` with `### Day N` subsections. Each day: **Goal**, **Deliverables (file paths)**, **SDs landed**, **Acceptance criteria** (yes/no), **Tests added**.

   - **Day 1 — Index foundation + IDs.** Deliverables: `artagents/threads/{ids,index,schema}.py` with `fcntl.flock` + tmp+fsync+`os.replace` + `.bak` rotate (SD-003), Crockford ULIDs (SD-004), `schema_version: 1` with N-1 reader (SD-039), repo-relative paths only (SD-040). Acceptance: 8 parallel writes preserve all updates; corrupted file recovers from `.bak`. Tests: `tests/test_threads_index.py` (schema round-trip, lock contention, partial-write recovery).

   - **Day 2 — `run.json` + chokepoint wrapper + CLI/request plumbing.** Deliverables:
     - `artagents/threads/{record,wrapper}.py` writing all SD-008 fields (incl. empty-but-present `parent_run_ids`, `chosen_from_groups`, `external_service_calls`, `output_artifacts`).
     - **Modify `ExecutorRunRequest` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:43`) to add `thread: str | None = None`, `variants: int | None = None`, `from_variant: str | None = None`.** Mirror in `OrchestratorRunRequest` in `artagents/orchestrators/runner.py`.
     - **Modify `artagents/executors/cli.py` `_cmd_run` (`artagents/executors/cli.py:170`) and the `run_parser` setup near `artagents/executors/cli.py:63-79` to add `--thread <id|@new|@none>`, `--variants N`, `--from <run-id>:<n>` and pass them into `ExecutorRunRequest`.** Mirror in `artagents/orchestrators/cli.py` `_cmd_run` and the orchestrator `run_parser`.
     - Wrap `run_executor()` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:75`) and the orchestrator runner with `threads.begin(request)` / `threads.finalize(record_id, result)` per SD-030.
     - No-op gates: `dry_run=True`, `out` under `tempfile.gettempdir()` or unwritable, `request.thread == "@none"`, `ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1`.
     - SD-008 fields: `host_id` per SD-012, `agent_version` from cached `git rev-parse HEAD`, `inputs_digest` via `xxhash`, `output_artifacts[*].sha256` at finalize (SD-032), `external_service_calls` capturing `model`/`model_version`/`request_id`/`seed`/`cost_usd`/`latency_ms` (SD-010), `input_artifacts.produced_by_run_id` resolved at consumption time (SD-011).
     - **SD-041 hooks (Day 2 portion):** `cli_args_redacted` strips values whose argv key matches `/(?i)^secret[_-]?/` or whose source env var matches the same pattern; `runs/<slug>/private/` is treated as opaque (briefs and artifacts under it produce sha-only `input_artifacts` entries with `kind: "private"` and no plaintext copy).
     - Acceptance: `executors run --thread @new …` and `orchestrators run --thread @new …` both create a new thread and emit `run.json` with all SD-008 fields populated; `--thread @none` produces no `run.json` and no `.artagents/` write; `ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1` is identical to `@none`; `cli_args_redacted` excludes any `--secret*` or `secret_*` value; orchestrator nested executor inherits parent thread via env. Tests: `tests/test_threads_record.py` (SD-008 field presence), `tests/test_threads_cli_plumbing.py` (`--thread`/`--variants`/`--from` reach the wrapper for both `executors run` and `orchestrators run`), `tests/test_threads_redaction.py` (SD-041 secret stripping).

   - **Day 3 — Auto-attribute + prefix lines + lifecycle CLI + reaper.** Deliverables: `artagents/threads/{attribute,prefix}.py`, 5-branch decision per SD-006 (lineage inference scans args for `runs/<R>/` paths) evaluated under lock, prefix lines per SD-018 + OQ-3 format with tiered Notice/Warn (first-run-of-process, gap >1h, brief novelty, auto-reopen), `python3 -m artagents thread {new,list,show,archive,reopen}` per SD-007 with `IDLE_ARCHIVE_WINDOW=7d`, `IDLE_REOPEN_WINDOW=48h`. **`thread show` accepts `--no-content` per SD-041** (displays only hashes/labels for fields under `runs/<slug>/private/` or whose key matches the secret prefix). In-flight reaper (SD-033) lazy-on-first-index-read per process. Acceptance: prefix line printed BEFORE rest of stdout; archive→reopen window honored; reaper marks orphan `returncode: -1, status: "orphaned"`; `thread show --no-content` does not print plaintext briefs. Tests: `tests/test_threads_attribute.py` (5 branches incl. lineage, frozen clock), `tests/test_threads_prefix.py`, `tests/test_threads_reaper.py`, `tests/test_threads_no_content.py`.

   - **Day 4 — Variants primitive + selection (with last-write-wins documentation).** Deliverables:
     - Extend `output_artifact` schema with `role`/`group`/`group_index`/`preview_modes`/`duration`/`variant_meta` (SD-013, SD-026).
     - `artagents/threads/selections.py` writing append-only `.artagents/threads/<thread-id>/selections.jsonl` per SD-014 with line-buffered atomic appends (no fcntl needed per SD-042).
     - `artagents/threads/groups.py` writing denormalized `groups.json` per SD-016, updated on run-write and selection-write.
     - `chosen_from_groups` edge writer per SD-015 (separate from `parent_run_ids`).
     - `thread keep <run-id>:<n>[,<n>|none]` and `thread group <run-id> ...` CLI per SD-017 (exactly five gestures total).
     - `[variants]` nag per SD-018, emitted only when unresolved variants exist.
     - **SD-042 documentation hook (Day 4):** `[variants]` line help text and the help banner of `thread keep` include the verbatim sentence: "selections are append-only; the most recent write is authoritative on read; prior selections are preserved as history but do not affect current keepers." (Same sentence is added to `SKILL.md` on Day 5.)
     - Executor patches per SD-031: `artagents/executors/generate_image/run.py` declares `role: variant`, `group = sha256(run_id + prompt_index)[:16]`, populates `preview_modes` and `duration`; `artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py` folds `name`/`rationale`/`prompt`/`generated.*` into `variant_meta`. **No other executor `run.py` is modified.**
     - Acceptance: heterogeneous output (4 variant images + grid + manifest) groups only the 4 variants; `groups.json` reduces correctly over a multi-event `selections.jsonl` (last-write-wins on read); `chosen_from_groups` is a separate edge from `parent_run_ids`; `thread keep --help` shows the SD-042 sentence verbatim. Tests: `tests/test_threads_variants.py` (heterogeneous grouping, replay, concurrent-pick LWW), `tests/test_threads_variants_help.py` (SD-042 string presence).

   - **Day 5 — Brief snapshot + provenance + redaction-aware docs + Week-1 polish.** Deliverables:
     - Brief snapshotting (SD-009): `runs/<slug>/brief.copy.txt` + `brief_content_sha256`. **SD-041 brief opt-in:** if the source brief lives under `runs/<slug>/private/` or its caller passes `--brief-private`, the snapshot copy is written under `runs/<slug>/private/brief.copy.txt` and `run.json` records only the sha and `kind: "private"` for the entry; default is plaintext.
     - Provenance block in `hype.metadata.json` added by `builtin.cut` per SD-019 (denormalize `thread_label`, `agent_version`, `starred`).
     - **`docs/threads.md` per SD-038**, including: an explicit `## Privacy & Redaction` section documenting `runs/` is gitignored / local-only, the `secret`-prefixed env convention, the `runs/<slug>/private/` brief opt-in, and `thread show --no-content` (SD-041); a `## Concurrent Variant Selection` section quoting the SD-042 last-write-wins sentence; a one-paragraph "deferred features" pointer to SD-036.
     - One paragraph appended to `SKILL.md` per SD-038 instructing agents to run `thread show @active` at session start, **plus the SD-042 last-write-wins sentence verbatim**.
     - Footer line in `executors inspect` / `orchestrators inspect` naming the active thread (SD-038).
     - `python3 -m artagents thread backfill` per SD-034 (cluster orphan dirs by hash-graph connected components).
     - Acceptance: every SD-008 field is now produced for every run; `hype.metadata.json` carries the SD-019 block; `thread_label` survives `rm -rf .artagents/`; private-brief snapshot writes only to `runs/<slug>/private/`; full pytest suite passes unmodified. Tests: `tests/test_threads_provenance.py`, `tests/test_threads_off.py` (`ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1` no-op), `tests/test_threads_backfill.py`, `tests/test_threads_brief_private.py`.

### Step 5: Author Week 2 — Iteration Video MVP days 6–10 (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** `## Week 2: Iteration Video MVP (Days 6–10)`:

   - **Day 6 — Modality registry + `iteration.collect`.** Deliverables: `artagents/modalities/__init__.py` (registry per SD-025), `artagents/modalities/{image_grid,audio_waveform,generic_card}.py` declaring `kinds`, `clip_modes`, `default_clip_mode_for(shape, style)`, `produces_audio`, `cost_hint`; `python3 -m artagents modalities {list, inspect}`; `artagents/executors/iteration_collect/` walks `parent_run_ids` backward (provenance-graph default per SD-023), computes `data_quality` per the **corrected OQ-6 formula** (valid roots count toward parent_capture_score). Acceptance: `iteration.quality.json` enumerates each run's missing signals and distinguishes valid roots from unresolved-producer entries; only 3 renderers register; `generic_card` is always last in the fallback chain.

   - **Day 7 — `iteration.summarize` + `iteration.score` + summary cache (SD-043).** Deliverables: `artagents/executors/iteration_summarize/` calling `builtin.understand` via `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4)` per OQ-2; brief-diff captions read from `runs/<slug>/brief.copy.txt` (or sha-only when `private/`). **SD-043 cache:** `.artagents/iteration_cache/<run_id>__<summarizer_model_version>.json` is read before any `builtin.understand` call; on cache miss summarize then write; on cache hit skip. `artagents/executors/iteration_score/` produces deterministic ordering by causal depth + selection-event tiebreaks. Acceptance: 12 parallel summarize calls finish in <4× single-call wallclock; re-running iteration_video on a thread that grew by 3 runs only summarizes the 3 new runs (cache hits visible in stdout); score output is deterministic for a given input set. Tests: `tests/test_iteration_summarize_cache.py`, `tests/test_iteration_score.py`.

   - **Day 8 — `iteration.assemble` + quality floor.** Deliverables: `artagents/executors/iteration_assemble/` resolves `(kind, preview_modes)` → renderer per SD-026, applies precedence `theme > direction > style preset > defaults` per SD-027, audio-bed selection per SD-028 (>40% audio coverage → iterations-as-bed; theme bed; silence + room tone; **never generative music**); emits `iteration.timeline.json`. Quality floor refusal per SD-024: when `data_quality < 0.6` print actionable report listing exact `thread …` backfill commands for each unresolved-producer run; `--force` bypass logged into provenance. Acceptance: refusal report names specific runs (and never names valid roots); bypassed render carries `forced: true` in `iteration.report.html` provenance. Tests: `tests/test_iteration_assemble.py`, `tests/test_quality_floor.py` (incl. root-heavy thread does NOT refuse).

   - **Day 9 — `builtin.iteration_video` orchestrator + report + loud fallback + cost surface (SD-043).** Deliverables: `artagents/orchestrators/iteration_video/` chaining `iteration.collect → iteration.summarize → iteration.score → iteration.assemble → builtin.cut → builtin.render` per SD-021; outputs the SD-022 5-tuple as a single variant group (`iteration.mp4`, `iteration.timeline.json`, `iteration.manifest.json`, `iteration.report.html`, `iteration.quality.json`); 5 flags + 1 inspect command per SD-027 (`--renderers`, `--clip-mode`, `--direction` accepted as label only per SD-036, `--mode` defaulting to `chaptered` (parallel/interleaved deferred), `--audio-bed`); `iteration_video inspect <thread>` prints detected modalities, chosen renderers, defaults. **SD-043 cost surface:** `inspect` prints an `Estimated cost:` line summing `(N_uncached_runs * per_call_cost_estimate)` using the configured summarizer's known cost; **`max_iterations` cap (default 200) refuses with an actionable message naming the cap and the override flag** when N_runs exceeds the cap. SD-029 loud fallback: `generic_card` use produces both a `<aside class="renderer-fallback">no renderer for kind:&lt;X&gt;</aside>` annotation in `iteration.report.html` and a stdout `WARN:` line. **`iteration.report.html` honors `--no-content` per SD-041** (renders sha and label only for private content). Acceptance: `inspect runs/artagents_logo_v3-thread` lists `image` modality + `image_grid` renderer + an estimated-cost line; injecting a `kind: model_3d` artifact triggers both warnings; running on a synthetic 250-iteration thread refuses with the SD-043 cap message. Tests: `tests/test_iteration_video_inspect.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_cost_cap.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_no_content.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_fallback.py`.

   - **Day 10 — Dogfood + integration suite + buffer.** Deliverables: end-to-end render of `runs/artagents_logo_v3` (after Day 5 backfill adopts it) producing all 5 SD-022 outputs with `data_quality ≥ 0.6`; full SD-037 integration suite green; cleanup of week's TODOs. Acceptance: `iteration.mp4` plays; `iteration.report.html` lists 4 logo variants and the chosen one with brief-diff captions; full `pytest` green; SD-037 tests all green.

### Step 6: Author Critical Path + Risks (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Critical Path`: Day-1 atomic write → Day-2 wrapper + CLI plumbing → Day-3 auto-attribute → Day-4 variants → Day-5 brief+provenance+docs → Day-6 collect → Day-7 summarize+cache → Day-8 assemble+quality floor → Day-9 orchestrator+cost surface → Day-10 dogfood. Day 5 is the latest date for SD-008 metadata; slipping it pollutes the run corpus permanently. Day 2 CLI plumbing is on the critical path because Day 4 variant gestures and Day 3 prefix lines depend on `--thread`/`--variants`/`--from` reaching the wrapper.
2. **Add** `## Risks and Mitigations` (≥6 risks):
   - Lock contention under SD-037 8-parallel test → short critical sections, exponential-backoff retry, lock-time logging.
   - Chokepoint regression breaking existing tests → wrapper no-op gates (SD-030); full pytest gated each day.
   - `runs/artagents_logo_v3` insufficient as fixture (e.g., `data_quality < 0.6`) → Day 5 backfill must adopt it; if floor fails, `--force` plus annotated report still demonstrates loud-fallback path.
   - Schema bug found post-Day-2 → SD-039 N-1 migration path built Day 1; remediation = bump schema_version + ship migrator.
   - `builtin.understand` rate-limits under parallel summarize → max_workers=4 + retry-with-backoff + `ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_SEQUENTIAL=1` fallback + SD-043 cache reduces N.
   - **Privacy regression (SD-041)**: agent shares a thread that contains plaintext briefs from `runs/<slug>/` → `thread show --no-content` is the documented sharing path; private convention is documented Day 5; redaction tests gate Day 2.
   - **Cost blowup (SD-043)**: 500-iteration thread incurs $50+ summarize bill silently → `max_iterations=200` cap refuses by default; cost estimate printed by `inspect` before render.
   - Scope creep into SD-036 cuts → any deferred-feature ask gets logged to `docs/threads.md#deferred` and bounced to follow-up.

### Step 7: Author Integration Test Plan + Dogfood Plan (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `## Integration Test Plan` rolling up SD-037: one bullet per SD-037 line item mapped to a concrete test file (`tests/test_threads_index.py`, `tests/test_threads_attribute.py`, `tests/test_threads_concurrent.py`, `tests/test_threads_record.py`, `tests/test_threads_cli_plumbing.py`, `tests/test_threads_variants.py`, `tests/test_threads_provenance.py`, `tests/test_threads_off.py`, `tests/test_threads_backfill.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video.py`). Add SD-041/042/043 coverage rows: `tests/test_threads_redaction.py`, `tests/test_threads_no_content.py`, `tests/test_threads_brief_private.py`, `tests/test_threads_variants_help.py`, `tests/test_iteration_summarize_cache.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_cost_cap.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_no_content.py`. Daily-regression gate: full `pytest` runs at end of each day, must be green.
2. **Add** `## Dogfood Plan: runs/artagents_logo_v3`:
   - Day 5: `python3 -m artagents thread backfill` adopts `runs/artagents_logo_v3` into a thread; verify `concepts.json`, `grid.jpg`, `logo-manifest.json`, `prompts.json`, `images/logo-00N.png` reflected in synthesized `run.json` with roles correctly assigned (variant for `images/logo-00N.png`, ancillary for `grid.jpg`/`logo-manifest.json`/`logo-plan.json`/`concepts.json`/`prompts.json`).
   - Day 9: `iteration_video inspect <thread>` lists `image` + `image_grid` + estimated-cost line; manually inject `kind: model_3d` artifact to verify SD-029 loud-fallback path; verify `--no-content` strips plaintext.
   - Day 10: render. Expected outputs: `iteration.mp4` (~10–20s), `iteration.timeline.json`, `iteration.manifest.json`, `iteration.report.html` (carries SD-019 provenance + brief-diff captions), `iteration.quality.json` (≥0.6).
   - Manual checklist: video plays; HTML report opens; "in-thread" vs "pulled-by-ancestry" labels present; `chosen_from_groups` reflects kept variant; cost line appears in `inspect`; `--no-content` mode shows hashes only.

### Step 8: Author closing sections (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Out of Scope` reproducing SD-036 verbatim plus SD-044 note that `thread`-vs-other-nouns and scalar `thread_id`-vs-DAG-membership are recorded tradeoffs (non-load-bearing context).
2. **Add** `## Documentation Deliverables` mapping SD-038 to: `docs/threads.md` (Day 5; includes SD-041 Privacy & SD-042 Concurrent Selection sections), `SKILL.md` paragraph (Day 5; includes SD-042 last-write-wins sentence), inspect-footer text (Day 5).
3. **Add** `## Definition of Done`: (a) all SD-008 fields produced for every run after Day 2; (b) SD-037 test suite green; (c) full pytest green; (d) dogfood produces all 5 SD-022 outputs; (e) `docs/threads.md` and SKILL.md updated; (f) zero modifications to executor `run.py` outside SD-031's two named files; (g) `--no-content` works on `thread show` and `iteration.report.html` (SD-041); (h) SD-042 last-write-wins text present in SKILL.md and `thread keep --help`; (i) `iteration_video inspect` prints estimated cost and `max_iterations=200` cap is enforced (SD-043).

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1–2 (exec summary + constraints) first.
2. Step 3 (OQ resolutions, including the corrected OQ-6) before day-by-day, since OQ-6 feeds Day 6 and Day 8.
3. Step 4 (Week 1) before Step 5 (Week 2) — Week 2 depends on Week 1.
4. Step 6 (critical path + risks) after both weeks scoped.
5. Step 7 (integration + dogfood) — aggregates concrete file paths from Steps 4–5.
6. Step 8 (closing) last.

## Validation Order
1. Confirm every load-bearing SD (SD-001…SD-043) appears in some day's "SDs landed" line or in scope/cuts.
2. Confirm SD-041 (`--no-content`, `runs/<slug>/private/`, secret env stripping), SD-042 (last-write-wins sentence in SKILL.md and `[variants]`/`thread keep` help), and SD-043 (`max_iterations` cap, summary cache, `inspect` cost line) each appear with concrete file paths and acceptance criteria.
3. Confirm OQ-6 formula does NOT penalize valid roots (input_artifacts == []).
4. Confirm Day 2 explicitly schedules `ExecutorRunRequest`/`OrchestratorRunRequest` field additions and `run_parser` `--thread`/`--variants`/`--from` flags for both `executors run` and `orchestrators run`.
5. Confirm all 6 OQs are explicitly resolved.
6. Confirm exec summary fits ~one page.
7. Confirm dogfood names exact files in `runs/artagents_logo_v3/`.
8. Confirm only `docs/sprint-thread-layer.md` is authored.

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

- ADDITIONAL GAPS (orchestrator review of plan_v1.md). Treat each as required for v2:

1) Day 3 is overpacked. Auto-attribute (5-branch decision per SD-006 including lineage inference), prefix lines (SD-018), 5 CLI subcommands (thread new/list/show/archive/reopen), lifecycle (SD-007), and lazy reaper (SD-033) is realistically 8-10 hours of focused work. The only buffer is Day 10 shared with dogfood. Either (a) move the lazy reaper and one CLI subcommand (archive/reopen) to Day 4 morning, or (b) explicitly mark Day 3 as a 'risk day' with a named cut-list if it slips past 6pm.

2) Documentation timing is wrong in Step 4. SKILL.md paragraph + 'executors inspect' / 'orchestrators inspect' footer (SD-038) are scheduled for Day 5, but agents will start seeing the [thread] prefix on Day 3. The footer and SKILL.md paragraph must land Day 3 alongside the prefix lines so an agent reading tool output on Day 3 has docs that explain what they're seeing. Only docs/threads.md (the longer reference) belongs Day 5.

3) Add a 'Frozen Schema Review' checkpoint at end-of-Day-2. Once SD-008 fields ship, any change requires SD-039 migration support. The plan should make this an explicit milestone (literally a line in Day 2 acceptance: 'schema_version=1 frozen; any later change requires migration helper + .bak rotation test added before the change merges').

4) _run_upload_youtube bypass at artagents/executors/runner.py:78 short-circuits _run_executor_inner. SD-030's 'no-op when out is unwritable' covers it but the integration test plan must include a test that confirms upload.youtube produces zero thread artifacts and zero errors.

5) Nested-executor env threading is real plumbing not scheduled. SD-030 says 'children skip begin if ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID env is set'. This requires modifying _run_external_executor's subprocess env at runner.py:258 to thread ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID through. Schedule on Day 3 (alongside the wrapper expansion) and add a test in tests/test_threads_nested.py that verifies parent->child inheritance.

6) Secret/env-var redaction in cli_args_redacted (SD-008) needs a Day 2 scrub even before SD-041's broader privacy work lands. Basic rule: any CLI arg whose value matches an env-var-style pattern containing KEY/TOKEN/SECRET/PASSWORD gets the value replaced with '***REDACTED***'. Schedule as a 30-min Day 2 task with a unit test.

7) The Risks section is too generic. For each named risk add a one-line concrete mitigation. Specifically: 'Lock contention' -> 'fcntl 30s timeout, error message points at thread gc --force-unlock command'; 'builtin.understand rate limits' -> 'ThreadPoolExecutor max_workers configurable via env var ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_CONCURRENCY default 4, with exponential backoff per call'; 'Schema bug post-Day-2' -> 'SD-039 migration helper added before any field change; .bak rotation test required'.

8) Add a Day 10 'demo milestone' as an acceptance criterion. Not just 'render works' but 'produce a 60-second iteration video against runs/artagents_logo_v3, screen-share it, manual checklist signed off'. The dogfood is the demo.

These are additive to the gate's 3 items already being addressed in revise. Land all 8 (gate's 3 + these 5 not already overlapping) before finalize.
- AGENT UX LAYER GAPS (orchestrator review). The plan currently treats agent UX as deliverables-to-land rather than a UX surface to design. Add an explicit '## Agent UX Surface' subsection to the sprint plan with day-tagged commitments addressing all of the following:

1) TIERED NOTICE/WARN DISCIPLINE. SD-018 names three tiers (info / notice / warn) but the plan must commit to firing rules: Info = every run; Notice = first run of process, gap >1h, cwd change, auto-reopen of archived thread; Warn = brief-novelty against thread history (parsable, rare). Acceptance criterion: 'Warn tier fires <5% of runs in dogfood or the trigger thresholds get tuned before Day 10.' Without this discipline the implementation drifts into warning-spam and agents learn to ignore the lines.

2) [HINT] LINE for suspected manual fan-out (3+ sequential runs of the same executor with no --variants flag) suggesting 'thread group <ids>'. Schedule on Day 4 alongside the [variants] nag. Soft nudge, not warning.

3) [THREAD-HEALTH] SMELL LINE on 'thread list' output (e.g., '12 variant groups, 0 kept' or '3 open threads idle >5d'). Schedule Day 3 with the lifecycle CLI.

4) SKILL.MD ONE-SENTENCE WORDING IS LOAD-BEARING. Every agent will pattern off it. The plan must specify the exact text to land on Day 3, not 'add a paragraph'. Suggested anchor: 'At the start of any session that will produce runs, run python3 -m artagents thread show @active first. The [thread] prefix on every command output is your continuous indicator; if it shows the wrong thread, run thread new or pass --thread @new.' Plan can refine but must commit to the exact wording.

5) OUTPUT-FORMAT STABILITY COMMITMENT. The [thread] and [variants] prefix lines are part of the public CLI contract. Add to Definition of Done: 'prefix line format governed by schema_version; format changes require a major version bump and a transition period.' Agents that pattern-match break silently otherwise.

6) LINE-ORDERING RULE when [thread], [variants], Notice, and Warn all fire on the same run. Specify: stream (stdout for all, before any other output); order (thread, variants, notice, warn, then command output); separator (single blank line between prefix block and command output). Trivial to specify on Day 2, expensive to fix later.

7) ITERATION_VIDEO INSPECT AS PRIMARY DISCOVERY TOOL. The plan treats it as one subcommand among five but it is THE gesture an agent should run before any iteration-video render (per the design's agent-UX reasoning). The SKILL.md text or a separate iteration-video doc must instruct: 'Always run iteration_video inspect <thread> before iteration_video run; it shows detected modalities, chosen renderers, estimated cost, and quality score so you can adjust before committing to a render.'

8) COST ESTIMATE SURFACING per SD-043. iteration_video inspect must print estimated cost (= summarize_calls * model_cost + render_cost_estimate) BEFORE the agent commits to a render. Schedule on Day 6 with iteration.collect (which has the run count) or Day 9 with the orchestrator wiring. Acceptance: 'inspect output includes a Cost section with low/high range and a one-line recommendation.'

9) THE FAILURE-MODE ACCEPTANCE CRITERION (most important). An agent that forgets threads exist will silently lump everything into one giant pile and the killer demo dies. Add to Day 10 dogfood acceptance: 'verify that during dogfood the [thread] prefix appeared on the stdout of every executors run / orchestrators run invocation, that thread show @active was called by the agent before iteration_video, and that no run was attributed to thread @active when a more recent thread had been opened (lineage inference worked).' Make it observable, not assumed.

10) DOCUMENT WHEN EACH TIER FIRES in docs/threads.md so agents reading the doc can predict what they'll see. Day 5 deliverable, scoped to one short table.

These items are load-bearing for adoption: if the agent UX is sloppy, the data the iteration video reads is sloppy, and the demo lies. They are not deferrable to a follow-up sprint.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Sprint Plan Document for Thread/Variant/Iteration-Video Layer

## Overview
Produce `docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`: a 2-week (10 working day) sprint plan that turns `docs/design-thread-layer.md`'s Settled Decisions (SD-001…SD-044) into day-by-day deliverables. The deliverable is a planning document, not code. SD-035 fixes the week split, SD-036 fixes scope cuts. SD-041 (privacy/redaction), SD-042 (concurrent variant-selection docs), and SD-043 (iteration-video cost guardrail) are load-bearing and must each land on a specific day with acceptance criteria. SD-044 is non-load-bearing context.

Document sections required: one-page exec summary, day-by-day plan with per-day acceptance criteria, critical path, risks + mitigations, integration test plan (rolling up SD-037), dogfood plan against `runs/artagents_logo_v3`, and explicit resolutions to the six Open Questions.

Repo grounding (verified):
- Chokepoint: `artagents/executors/runner.py:75` (`run_executor`) and `artagents/orchestrators/runner.py`.
- `ExecutorRunRequest` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:43-54`) and `OrchestratorRunRequest` have **no** `thread` field today; `_cmd_run` in `artagents/executors/cli.py:170` and the orchestrator `_cmd_run` build the requests from `--out`/`--input`/`--brief`/`--dry-run`/`--python-exec`/`--verbose` only — no `--thread`/`--variants`/`--from`. CLI plumbing is an explicit Day-2 prerequisite.
- Variant-producing tools: `artagents/executors/generate_image/run.py`, `artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py`.
- Fixture: `runs/artagents_logo_v3/` with `concepts.json`, `grid.jpg`, `logo-manifest.json`, `logo-plan.json`, `prompts.json`, `images/logo-00N.png`.
- `requirements.txt` already declares OpenAI/scenedetect/anthropic/google-genai/python-dotenv/filelock/jsonschema/pillow/fal-client; **does not yet declare `xxhash`** — Day 1 must add it. There is no `pyproject.toml`, so `requirements.txt` is the canonical location.
- `tests/` is pytest-based; existing coverage must remain green (SD-034, SD-037).
- `SKILL.md` exists at repo root (touchpoint for SD-038 paragraph and SD-042 last-write-wins documentation).

Approach assessment vs. flags: none of the open flags suggest the plan is targeting the wrong code or wrong root cause. All three remaining concerns are narrow corrections — drop the invented `--brief-private` flag, relocate the `max_iterations` cap into the `iteration.summarize` executor (where SD-043 puts it), and add `xxhash` to `requirements.txt`. Plan shape stands.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Author the exec summary (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Open** the document with `# Sprint Plan: Thread / Variant / Iteration-Video Layer`.
2. **Write** a one-page `## Executive Summary` (~40 lines) covering: goal, why-now (SD-008 irreversibility), scope-in (SD-035 + SD-041/042/043), scope-out (SD-036 verbatim), critical path one-liner, top 3 risks with one-line mitigations, definition-of-done. Include one sentence each on SD-041 redaction, SD-042 last-write-wins, SD-043 cost guardrail.

### Step 2: Author Hard Constraints recap (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Hard Constraints (from design)` mapping each load-bearing SD (SD-001…SD-043, excluding non-load-bearing SD-044) to the day it lands. Group by week and call out SD-041, SD-042, SD-043 explicitly.
2. **Add** the fail-closed reminder: default `role: ancillary`; only `generate_image` and `logo_ideas` opt in to `variant` per SD-031.

### Step 3: Author Open Question resolutions (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Open Question Resolutions`. Resolve all 6 OQs:
   - **OQ-1 day allocation** → see Week 1/2 sections below.
   - **OQ-2 summarize parallelism** → `concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4)` (I/O-bound; matches existing repo patterns; avoids asyncio rewrite).
   - **OQ-3 prefix line format** → width `min(120, $COLUMNS)`, ANSI color iff `sys.stdout.isatty()` and `$NO_COLOR` unset, label truncated to 32 chars with `…`, never truncate IDs.
   - **OQ-4 reaper timing** → lazy on first index read per process (matches SD-033 wording).
   - **OQ-5 docs path** → `docs/threads.md` (matches SD-038).
   - **OQ-6 quality_floor formula** → distinguish valid roots from missing parent capture per SD-005. Formula: `data_quality = 0.5 * parent_capture_score + 0.3 * fraction_with_brief_content_sha256 + 0.2 * fraction_with_at_least_one_input_artifact_resolved`, where `parent_capture_score = (runs_with_parents + valid_roots) / total_runs` and a run is a `valid_root` iff `input_artifacts == []`. A run with non-empty `input_artifacts` but empty `parent_run_ids` (= unresolved producer hash) is the only signal that counts against parent capture. Default floor 0.6 per SD-024. Refusal report names the specific runs whose `input_artifacts` did not resolve to a producer (and never names valid roots).

### Step 4: Author Week 1 — Data Layer days 1–5 (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** `## Week 1: Data Layer (Days 1–5)` with `### Day N` subsections. Each day: **Goal**, **Deliverables (file paths)**, **SDs landed**, **Acceptance criteria** (yes/no), **Tests added**.

   - **Day 1 — Index foundation + IDs + dependency glue.** Deliverables: `artagents/threads/{ids,index,schema}.py` with `fcntl.flock` + tmp+fsync+`os.replace` + `.bak` rotate (SD-003), Crockford ULIDs (SD-004), `schema_version: 1` with N-1 reader (SD-039), repo-relative paths only (SD-040). **Append `xxhash` (with a known-stable version specifier such as `xxhash>=3.4`) to `requirements.txt`** as the canonical dependency declaration (no `pyproject.toml` exists in this repo); the schema module imports `xxhash` directly with no fallback so SD-032 always uses the fast non-cryptographic path. Acceptance: 8 parallel writes preserve all updates; corrupted file recovers from `.bak`; `pip install -r requirements.txt` resolves cleanly in a fresh venv and `import xxhash` succeeds. Tests: `tests/test_threads_index.py` (schema round-trip, lock contention, partial-write recovery), `tests/test_threads_dependencies.py` (asserts `xxhash` is importable and listed in `requirements.txt`).

   - **Day 2 — `run.json` + chokepoint wrapper + CLI/request plumbing.** Deliverables:
     - `artagents/threads/{record,wrapper}.py` writing all SD-008 fields (incl. empty-but-present `parent_run_ids`, `chosen_from_groups`, `external_service_calls`, `output_artifacts`).
     - **Modify `ExecutorRunRequest` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:43`) to add `thread: str | None = None`, `variants: int | None = None`, `from_variant: str | None = None`.** Mirror in `OrchestratorRunRequest` in `artagents/orchestrators/runner.py`.
     - **Modify `artagents/executors/cli.py` `_cmd_run` (`artagents/executors/cli.py:170`) and the `run_parser` setup (`artagents/executors/cli.py:63-79`) to add `--thread <id|@new|@none>`, `--variants N`, `--from <run-id>:<n>` and pass them into `ExecutorRunRequest`.** Mirror in `artagents/orchestrators/cli.py` `_cmd_run` and the orchestrator `run_parser`.
     - Wrap `run_executor()` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:75`) and the orchestrator runner with `threads.begin(request)` / `threads.finalize(record_id, result)` per SD-030.
     - No-op gates: `dry_run=True`, `out` under `tempfile.gettempdir()` or unwritable, `request.thread == "@none"`, `ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1`.
     - SD-008 fields: `host_id` per SD-012, `agent_version` from cached `git rev-parse HEAD`, `inputs_digest` via `xxhash` (Day-1 dependency), `output_artifacts[*].sha256` at finalize (SD-032), `external_service_calls` capturing `model`/`model_version`/`request_id`/`seed`/`cost_usd`/`latency_ms` (SD-010), `input_artifacts.produced_by_run_id` resolved at consumption time (SD-011).
     - **SD-041 hooks (Day 2 portion):** `cli_args_redacted` strips values whose argv key matches `/(?i)^secret[_-]?/` or whose source env var matches the same pattern; `runs/<slug>/private/` is treated as opaque (briefs and artifacts under that path produce sha-only `input_artifacts` entries with `kind: "private"` and no plaintext copy is recorded in `run.json`).
     - Acceptance: `executors run --thread @new …` and `orchestrators run --thread @new …` both create a new thread and emit `run.json` with all SD-008 fields populated; `--thread @none` produces no `run.json` and no `.artagents/` write; `ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1` is identical to `@none`; `cli_args_redacted` excludes any `--secret*` or `secret_*` value; orchestrator nested executor inherits parent thread via env. Tests: `tests/test_threads_record.py` (SD-008 field presence), `tests/test_threads_cli_plumbing.py` (`--thread`/`--variants`/`--from` reach the wrapper for both `executors run` and `orchestrators run`), `tests/test_threads_redaction.py` (SD-041 secret stripping).

   - **Day 3 — Auto-attribute + prefix lines + lifecycle CLI + reaper.** Deliverables: `artagents/threads/{attribute,prefix}.py`, 5-branch decision per SD-006 (lineage inference scans args for `runs/<R>/` paths) evaluated under lock, prefix lines per SD-018 + OQ-3 format with tiered Notice/Warn (first-run-of-process, gap >1h, brief novelty, auto-reopen), `python3 -m artagents thread {new,list,show,archive,reopen}` per SD-007 with `IDLE_ARCHIVE_WINDOW=7d`, `IDLE_REOPEN_WINDOW=48h`. **`thread show` accepts `--no-content` per SD-041** (displays only hashes/labels for fields under `runs/<slug>/private/` or whose key matches the secret prefix). In-flight reaper (SD-033) lazy-on-first-index-read per process. Acceptance: prefix line printed BEFORE rest of stdout; archive→reopen window honored; reaper marks orphan `returncode: -1, status: "orphaned"`; `thread show --no-content` does not print plaintext briefs. Tests: `tests/test_threads_attribute.py` (5 branches incl. lineage, frozen clock), `tests/test_threads_prefix.py`, `tests/test_threads_reaper.py`, `tests/test_threads_no_content.py`.

   - **Day 4 — Variants primitive + selection (with last-write-wins documentation).** Deliverables:
     - Extend `output_artifact` schema with `role`/`group`/`group_index`/`preview_modes`/`duration`/`variant_meta` (SD-013, SD-026).
     - `artagents/threads/selections.py` writing append-only `.artagents/threads/<thread-id>/selections.jsonl` per SD-014 with line-buffered atomic appends (no fcntl needed per SD-042).
     - `artagents/threads/groups.py` writing denormalized `groups.json` per SD-016, updated on run-write and selection-write.
     - `chosen_from_groups` edge writer per SD-015 (separate from `parent_run_ids`).
     - `thread keep <run-id>:<n>[,<n>|none]` and `thread group <run-id> ...` CLI per SD-017 (exactly five gestures total).
     - `[variants]` nag per SD-018, emitted only when unresolved variants exist.
     - **SD-042 documentation hook (Day 4):** `[variants]` line help text and the help banner of `thread keep` include the verbatim sentence: "selections are append-only; the most recent write is authoritative on read; prior selections are preserved as history but do not affect current keepers." (Same sentence is added to `SKILL.md` on Day 5.)
     - Executor patches per SD-031: `artagents/executors/generate_image/run.py` declares `role: variant`, `group = sha256(run_id + prompt_index)[:16]`, populates `preview_modes` and `duration`; `artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py` folds `name`/`rationale`/`prompt`/`generated.*` into `variant_meta`. **No other executor `run.py` is modified.**
     - Acceptance: heterogeneous output (4 variant images + grid + manifest) groups only the 4 variants; `groups.json` reduces correctly over a multi-event `selections.jsonl` (last-write-wins on read); `chosen_from_groups` is a separate edge from `parent_run_ids`; `thread keep --help` shows the SD-042 sentence verbatim. Tests: `tests/test_threads_variants.py` (heterogeneous grouping, replay, concurrent-pick LWW), `tests/test_threads_variants_help.py` (SD-042 string presence).

   - **Day 5 — Brief snapshot + provenance + redaction-aware docs + Week-1 polish.** Deliverables:
     - Brief snapshotting (SD-009): `runs/<slug>/brief.copy.txt` + `brief_content_sha256`. **SD-041 brief opt-in is path-based only**: when the resolved source brief path lives under `runs/<slug>/private/`, the snapshot copy is written under `runs/<slug>/private/brief.copy.txt` and `run.json` records only the sha and `kind: "private"` for the entry. Default (any other path) is plaintext snapshot. **No new user-facing flag is introduced** — the `runs/<slug>/private/` convention is the entire opt-in surface per SD-041.
     - Provenance block in `hype.metadata.json` added by `builtin.cut` per SD-019 (denormalize `thread_label`, `agent_version`, `starred`).
     - **`docs/threads.md` per SD-038**, including: a `## Privacy & Redaction` section documenting `runs/` is gitignored / local-only, the `secret`-prefixed env convention, the `runs/<slug>/private/` brief opt-in, and `thread show --no-content` (SD-041); a `## Concurrent Variant Selection` section quoting the SD-042 last-write-wins sentence; a one-paragraph "deferred features" pointer to SD-036.
     - One paragraph appended to `SKILL.md` per SD-038 instructing agents to run `thread show @active` at session start, **plus the SD-042 last-write-wins sentence verbatim**.
     - Footer line in `executors inspect` / `orchestrators inspect` naming the active thread (SD-038).
     - `python3 -m artagents thread backfill` per SD-034 (cluster orphan dirs by hash-graph connected components).
     - Acceptance: every SD-008 field is now produced for every run; `hype.metadata.json` carries the SD-019 block; `thread_label` survives `rm -rf .artagents/`; brief placed under `runs/<slug>/private/` produces sha-only `run.json` entry while plaintext briefs elsewhere snapshot normally; full pytest suite passes unmodified. Tests: `tests/test_threads_provenance.py`, `tests/test_threads_off.py` (`ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1` no-op), `tests/test_threads_backfill.py`, `tests/test_threads_brief_private.py` (asserts path-based opt-in only — there is no flag to test).

### Step 5: Author Week 2 — Iteration Video MVP days 6–10 (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** `## Week 2: Iteration Video MVP (Days 6–10)`:

   - **Day 6 — Modality registry + `iteration.collect`.** Deliverables: `artagents/modalities/__init__.py` (registry per SD-025), `artagents/modalities/{image_grid,audio_waveform,generic_card}.py` declaring `kinds`, `clip_modes`, `default_clip_mode_for(shape, style)`, `produces_audio`, `cost_hint`; `python3 -m artagents modalities {list, inspect}`; `artagents/executors/iteration_collect/` walks `parent_run_ids` backward (provenance-graph default per SD-023) and computes `data_quality` per the corrected OQ-6 formula (valid roots count toward parent_capture_score). Acceptance: `iteration.quality.json` enumerates each run's missing signals and distinguishes valid roots from unresolved-producer entries; only 3 renderers register; `generic_card` is always last in the fallback chain.

   - **Day 7 — `iteration.summarize` (with SD-043 cap inside the executor) + `iteration.score` + summary cache.** Deliverables:
     - `artagents/executors/iteration_summarize/` calling `builtin.understand` via `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4)` per OQ-2; brief-diff captions read from `runs/<slug>/brief.copy.txt` (or sha-only when the brief is under `private/`).
     - **SD-043 enforcement lives in this executor (per SD-043 wording: cap `iteration.summarize` at `max_iterations`).** Before any `builtin.understand` call is dispatched, count the input runs; if `count > max_iterations` (default 200, configurable via `--max-iterations` flag and `ARTAGENTS_ITERATION_MAX` env), **the executor refuses with a non-zero exit code and an actionable message naming the cap, the override flag, and the env var**. The cap is enforced unconditionally — direct `executors run iteration.summarize ...` invocations cannot bypass it.
     - **SD-043 cache:** `.artagents/iteration_cache/<run_id>__<summarizer_model_version>.json` is read before any `builtin.understand` call; on miss summarize then write; on hit skip. Cap is evaluated against the **uncached** count (cached runs do not consume budget).
     - `artagents/executors/iteration_score/` produces deterministic ordering by causal depth + selection-event tiebreaks.
     - Acceptance: 12 parallel summarize calls finish in <4× single-call wallclock; **`executors run iteration.summarize` directly on a 250-iteration set refuses with the SD-043 cap message before any `builtin.understand` call is dispatched**; re-running on a thread that grew by 3 runs only summarizes the 3 new runs (cache hits visible in stdout); score output is deterministic. Tests: `tests/test_iteration_summarize_cap.py` (executor-level refusal test invoked directly via `executors run`), `tests/test_iteration_summarize_cache.py`, `tests/test_iteration_score.py`.

   - **Day 8 — `iteration.assemble` + quality floor.** Deliverables: `artagents/executors/iteration_assemble/` resolves `(kind, preview_modes)` → renderer per SD-026, applies precedence `theme > direction > style preset > defaults` per SD-027, audio-bed selection per SD-028 (>40% audio coverage → iterations-as-bed; theme bed; silence + room tone; **never generative music**); emits `iteration.timeline.json`. Quality floor refusal per SD-024: when `data_quality < 0.6` print actionable report listing exact `thread …` backfill commands for each unresolved-producer run; `--force` bypass logged into provenance. Acceptance: refusal report names specific runs (and never names valid roots); bypassed render carries `forced: true` in `iteration.report.html` provenance. Tests: `tests/test_iteration_assemble.py`, `tests/test_quality_floor.py` (incl. root-heavy thread does NOT refuse).

   - **Day 9 — `builtin.iteration_video` orchestrator + report + loud fallback + cost preview (SD-043 UX layer).** Deliverables: `artagents/orchestrators/iteration_video/` chaining `iteration.collect → iteration.summarize → iteration.score → iteration.assemble → builtin.cut → builtin.render` per SD-021; outputs the SD-022 5-tuple as a single variant group (`iteration.mp4`, `iteration.timeline.json`, `iteration.manifest.json`, `iteration.report.html`, `iteration.quality.json`); 5 flags + 1 inspect command per SD-027 (`--renderers`, `--clip-mode`, `--direction` accepted as label only per SD-036, `--mode` defaulting to `chaptered` (parallel/interleaved deferred), `--audio-bed`); `iteration_video inspect <thread>` prints detected modalities, chosen renderers, defaults. **SD-043 UX preview (the cap itself remains in the Day-7 executor):** `inspect` prints an `Estimated cost:` line summing `(N_uncached_runs * per_call_cost_estimate)` using the configured summarizer's known cost, and **emits a clear warning when `N_runs > max_iterations`** so the agent sees the cap will trigger before invoking. `--max-iterations` is forwarded from the orchestrator down into `iteration.summarize`. SD-029 loud fallback: `generic_card` use produces both an `<aside class="renderer-fallback">no renderer for kind:&lt;X&gt;</aside>` annotation in `iteration.report.html` and a stdout `WARN:` line. **`iteration.report.html` honors `--no-content` per SD-041** (renders sha and label only for private content). Acceptance: `inspect runs/artagents_logo_v3-thread` lists `image` modality + `image_grid` renderer + an estimated-cost line; injecting a `kind: model_3d` artifact triggers both warnings; running orchestrator on a synthetic 250-iteration thread surfaces the warning during `inspect` and is refused at the executor layer (Day 7) when the orchestrator dispatches summarize. Tests: `tests/test_iteration_video_inspect.py` (cost line + cap warning), `tests/test_iteration_video_no_content.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_fallback.py`.

   - **Day 10 — Dogfood + integration suite + buffer.** Deliverables: end-to-end render of `runs/artagents_logo_v3` (after Day 5 backfill adopts it) producing all 5 SD-022 outputs with `data_quality ≥ 0.6`; full SD-037 integration suite green; cleanup of week's TODOs. Acceptance: `iteration.mp4` plays; `iteration.report.html` lists 4 logo variants and the chosen one with brief-diff captions; full `pytest` green; SD-037 tests all green.

### Step 6: Author Critical Path + Risks (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Critical Path`: Day-1 atomic write + xxhash dependency → Day-2 wrapper + CLI plumbing → Day-3 auto-attribute → Day-4 variants → Day-5 brief+provenance+docs → Day-6 collect → Day-7 summarize+cap+cache → Day-8 assemble+quality floor → Day-9 orchestrator+cost preview → Day-10 dogfood. Day 5 is the latest date for SD-008 metadata; slipping it pollutes the run corpus permanently. Day 1 dependency declaration is on the critical path because Day 2's `inputs_digest` requires `xxhash`. Day 2 CLI plumbing is on the critical path because Day-4 variant gestures and Day-3 prefix lines depend on `--thread`/`--variants`/`--from` reaching the wrapper. Day-7 cap is on the critical path because Day-9 orchestrator forwards `--max-iterations` into it.
2. **Add** `## Risks and Mitigations` (≥7 risks):
   - Lock contention under SD-037 8-parallel test → short critical sections, exponential-backoff retry, lock-time logging.
   - Chokepoint regression breaking existing tests → wrapper no-op gates (SD-030); full pytest gated each day.
   - `runs/artagents_logo_v3` insufficient as fixture (e.g., `data_quality < 0.6`) → Day 5 backfill must adopt it; if floor fails, `--force` plus annotated report still demonstrates the loud-fallback path.
   - Schema bug found post-Day-2 → SD-039 N-1 migration path built Day 1; remediation = bump schema_version + ship migrator.
   - `builtin.understand` rate-limits under parallel summarize → max_workers=4 + retry-with-backoff + `ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_SEQUENTIAL=1` fallback + SD-043 cache reduces N.
   - **Privacy regression (SD-041)**: agent shares a thread that contains plaintext briefs from `runs/<slug>/` → `thread show --no-content` is the documented sharing path; the `runs/<slug>/private/` convention is the entire opt-in surface (no flag); redaction tests gate Day 2.
   - **Cost blowup (SD-043)**: 500-iteration thread incurs $50+ summarize bill silently → `max_iterations=200` cap is enforced **inside `iteration.summarize` itself** (Day 7), so direct executor calls also refuse; `inspect` previews cost on Day 9.
   - **Dependency drift**: `xxhash` not pinned or absent in clean venv → Day-1 deliverable adds `xxhash>=3.4` to `requirements.txt` + `tests/test_threads_dependencies.py` asserts the import succeeds.
   - Scope creep into SD-036 cuts → any deferred-feature ask gets logged to `docs/threads.md#deferred` and bounced to follow-up.

### Step 7: Author Integration Test Plan + Dogfood Plan (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `## Integration Test Plan` rolling up SD-037: one bullet per SD-037 line item mapped to a concrete test file (`tests/test_threads_index.py`, `tests/test_threads_attribute.py`, `tests/test_threads_concurrent.py`, `tests/test_threads_record.py`, `tests/test_threads_cli_plumbing.py`, `tests/test_threads_variants.py`, `tests/test_threads_provenance.py`, `tests/test_threads_off.py`, `tests/test_threads_backfill.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video.py`). Add SD-041/042/043 + dependency rows: `tests/test_threads_redaction.py`, `tests/test_threads_no_content.py`, `tests/test_threads_brief_private.py`, `tests/test_threads_variants_help.py`, `tests/test_iteration_summarize_cap.py`, `tests/test_iteration_summarize_cache.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_no_content.py`, `tests/test_threads_dependencies.py`. Daily-regression gate: full `pytest` runs at end of each day, must be green.
2. **Add** `## Dogfood Plan: runs/artagents_logo_v3`:
   - Day 5: `python3 -m artagents thread backfill` adopts `runs/artagents_logo_v3` into a thread; verify `concepts.json`, `grid.jpg`, `logo-manifest.json`, `prompts.json`, `images/logo-00N.png` reflected in synthesized `run.json` with roles correctly assigned (variant for `images/logo-00N.png`, ancillary for `grid.jpg`/`logo-manifest.json`/`logo-plan.json`/`concepts.json`/`prompts.json`).
   - Day 9: `iteration_video inspect <thread>` lists `image` + `image_grid` + estimated-cost line; manually inject `kind: model_3d` artifact to verify SD-029 loud-fallback path; verify `--no-content` strips plaintext.
   - Day 10: render. Expected outputs: `iteration.mp4` (~10–20s), `iteration.timeline.json`, `iteration.manifest.json`, `iteration.report.html` (carries SD-019 provenance + brief-diff captions), `iteration.quality.json` (≥0.6).
   - Manual checklist: video plays; HTML report opens; "in-thread" vs "pulled-by-ancestry" labels present; `chosen_from_groups` reflects kept variant; cost line appears in `inspect`; `--no-content` mode shows hashes only.

### Step 8: Author closing sections (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Out of Scope` reproducing SD-036 verbatim plus an SD-044 note that `thread`-vs-other-nouns and scalar `thread_id`-vs-DAG-membership are recorded tradeoffs (non-load-bearing context).
2. **Add** `## Documentation Deliverables` mapping SD-038 to: `docs/threads.md` (Day 5; includes SD-041 Privacy & SD-042 Concurrent Selection sections), `SKILL.md` paragraph (Day 5; includes SD-042 last-write-wins sentence), inspect-footer text (Day 5).
3. **Add** `## Definition of Done`: (a) all SD-008 fields produced for every run after Day 2; (b) SD-037 test suite green; (c) full pytest green; (d) dogfood produces all 5 SD-022 outputs; (e) `docs/threads.md` and SKILL.md updated; (f) zero modifications to executor `run.py` outside SD-031's two named files; (g) `--no-content` works on `thread show` and `iteration.report.html` (SD-041) with **no extra brief flag invented** — opt-in is solely the `runs/<slug>/private/` path convention; (h) SD-042 last-write-wins text present in SKILL.md and `thread keep --help`; (i) `iteration.summarize` refuses at the executor layer when `N_runs > max_iterations` (SD-043) AND `iteration_video inspect` previews estimated cost; (j) `xxhash` is declared in `requirements.txt`.

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1–2 (exec summary + constraints) first.
2. Step 3 (OQ resolutions, including the corrected OQ-6) before day-by-day.
3. Step 4 (Week 1) before Step 5 (Week 2).
4. Step 6 (critical path + risks) after both weeks scoped.
5. Step 7 (integration + dogfood) — aggregates concrete file paths from Steps 4–5.
6. Step 8 (closing) last.

## Validation Order
1. Confirm every load-bearing SD (SD-001…SD-043) appears in some day's "SDs landed" line or in scope/cuts.
2. Confirm SD-041 work introduces **no new user-facing flag for brief privacy** — opt-in is solely `runs/<slug>/private/`. The only flags are `--no-content` (on `thread show` and `iteration_video`) per SD-041.
3. Confirm SD-043 `max_iterations` cap is enforced **inside `iteration.summarize` (Day 7)** so direct executor invocations also refuse, with the orchestrator/inspect surface acting as a UX preview only.
4. Confirm Day 1 schedules adding `xxhash` to `requirements.txt` (no `pyproject.toml` exists in this repo).
5. Confirm OQ-6 formula does NOT penalize valid roots (input_artifacts == []).
6. Confirm Day 2 explicitly schedules `ExecutorRunRequest`/`OrchestratorRunRequest` field additions and `run_parser` `--thread`/`--variants`/`--from` flags for both `executors run` and `orchestrators run`.
7. Confirm all 6 OQs are explicitly resolved.
8. Confirm exec summary fits ~one page.
9. Confirm dogfood names exact files in `runs/artagents_logo_v3/`.
10. Confirm only `docs/sprint-thread-layer.md` is authored.

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

This is critique iteration 3. The template file includes prior findings with their status. Verify addressed flags were actually fixed, re-flag if inadequate, and check for new issues introduced by the revision.

        Additional guidelines:
        - Robustness level: standard. Use balanced judgment. Flag significant risks, but do not spend flags on minor polish or executor-obvious boilerplate.
        - Over-engineering: prefer the simplest approach that fully solves the problem.
        - Reuse existing flag IDs when the same concern is still open.
        - `verified_flag_ids`: list flag IDs from prior iterations that the revised plan actually resolves (e.g., if the plan was revised to fix FLAG-001, and you confirm the fix is correct, include "FLAG-001"). Only include flags you've verified — don't guess.

        - Additional flags may use these categories: correctness, security, completeness, performance, maintainability, other.
        - Focus on concrete issues, not structural formatting.
