- ADDITIONAL GAPS (orchestrator review of plan_v1.md). Treat each as required for v2:

1) Day 3 is overpacked. Auto-attribute (5-branch decision per SD-006 including lineage inference), prefix lines (SD-018), 5 CLI subcommands (thread new/list/show/archive/reopen), lifecycle (SD-007), and lazy reaper (SD-033) is realistically 8-10 hours of focused work. The only buffer is Day 10 shared with dogfood. Either (a) move the lazy reaper and one CLI subcommand (archive/reopen) to Day 4 morning, or (b) explicitly mark Day 3 as a 'risk day' with a named cut-list if it slips past 6pm.

2) Documentation timing is wrong in Step 4. SKILL.md paragraph + 'executors inspect' / 'orchestrators inspect' footer (SD-038) are scheduled for Day 5, but agents will start seeing the [thread] prefix on Day 3. The footer and SKILL.md paragraph must land Day 3 alongside the prefix lines so an agent reading tool output on Day 3 has docs that explain what they're seeing. Only docs/threads.md (the longer reference) belongs Day 5.

3) Add a 'Frozen Schema Review' checkpoint at end-of-Day-2. Once SD-008 fields ship, any change requires SD-039 migration support. The plan should make this an explicit milestone (literally a line in Day 2 acceptance: 'schema_version=1 frozen; any later change requires migration helper + .bak rotation test added before the change merges').

4) _run_upload_youtube bypass at artagents/executors/runner.py:78 short-circuits _run_executor_inner. SD-030's 'no-op when out is unwritable' covers it but the integration test plan must include a test that confirms upload.youtube produces zero thread artifacts and zero errors.

5) Nested-executor env threading is real plumbing not scheduled. SD-030 says 'children skip begin if ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID env is set'. This requires modifying _run_external_executor's subprocess env at runner.py:258 to thread ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID through. Schedule on Day 3 (alongside the wrapper expansion) and add a test in tests/test_threads_nested.py that verifies parent->child inheritance.

6) Secret/env-var redaction in cli_args_redacted (SD-008) needs a Day 2 scrub even before SD-041's broader privacy work lands. Basic rule: any CLI arg whose value matches an env-var-style pattern containing KEY/TOKEN/SECRET/PASSWORD gets the value replaced with '***REDACTED***'. Schedule as a 30-min Day 2 task with a unit test.

7) The Risks section is too generic. For each named risk add a one-line concrete mitigation. Specifically: 'Lock contention' -> 'fcntl 30s timeout, error message points at thread gc --force-unlock command'; 'builtin.understand rate limits' -> 'ThreadPoolExecutor max_workers configurable via env var ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_CONCURRENCY default 4, with exponential backoff per call'; 'Schema bug post-Day-2' -> 'SD-039 migration helper added before any field change; .bak rotation test required'.

8) Add a Day 10 'demo milestone' as an acceptance criterion. Not just 'render works' but 'produce a 60-second iteration video against runs/artagents_logo_v3, screen-share it, manual checklist signed off'. The dogfood is the demo.

These are additive to the gate's 3 items already being addressed in revise. Land all 8 (gate's 3 + these 5 not already overlapping) before finalize.
- AGENT UX LAYER GAPS (orchestrator review). The plan currently treats agent UX as deliverables-to-land rather than a UX surface to design. Add an explicit '## Agent UX Surface' subsection to the sprint plan with day-tagged commitments addressing all of the following:

1) TIERED NOTICE/WARN DISCIPLINE. SD-018 names three tiers (info / notice / warn) but the plan must commit to firing rules: Info = every run; Notice = first run of process, gap >1h, cwd change, auto-reopen of archived thread; Warn = brief-novelty against thread history (parsable, rare). Acceptance criterion: 'Warn tier fires <5% of runs in dogfood or the trigger thresholds get tuned before Day 10.' Without this discipline the implementation drifts into warning-spam and agents learn to ignore the lines.

2) [HINT] LINE for suspected manual fan-out (3+ sequential runs of the same executor with no --variants flag) suggesting 'thread group <ids>'. Schedule on Day 4 alongside the [variants] nag. Soft nudge, not warning.

3) [THREAD-HEALTH] SMELL LINE on 'thread list' output (e.g., '12 variant groups, 0 kept' or '3 open threads idle >5d'). Schedule Day 3 with the lifecycle CLI.

4) SKILL.MD ONE-SENTENCE WORDING IS LOAD-BEARING. Every agent will pattern off it. The plan must specify the exact text to land on Day 3, not 'add a paragraph'. Suggested anchor: 'At the start of any session that will produce runs, run python3 -m artagents thread show @active first. The [thread] prefix on every command output is your continuous indicator; if it shows the wrong thread, run thread new or pass --thread @new.' Plan can refine but must commit to the exact wording.

5) OUTPUT-FORMAT STABILITY COMMITMENT. The [thread] and [variants] prefix lines are part of the public CLI contract. Add to Definition of Done: 'prefix line format governed by schema_version; format changes require a major version bump and a transition period.' Agents that pattern-match break silently otherwise.

6) LINE-ORDERING RULE when [thread], [variants], Notice, and Warn all fire on the same run. Specify: stream (stdout for all, before any other output); order (thread, variants, notice, warn, then command output); separator (single blank line between prefix block and command output). Trivial to specify on Day 2, expensive to fix later.

7) ITERATION_VIDEO INSPECT AS PRIMARY DISCOVERY TOOL. The plan treats it as one subcommand among five but it is THE gesture an agent should run before any iteration-video render (per the design's agent-UX reasoning). The SKILL.md text or a separate iteration-video doc must instruct: 'Always run iteration_video inspect <thread> before iteration_video run; it shows detected modalities, chosen renderers, estimated cost, and quality score so you can adjust before committing to a render.'

8) COST ESTIMATE SURFACING per SD-043. iteration_video inspect must print estimated cost (= summarize_calls * model_cost + render_cost_estimate) BEFORE the agent commits to a render. Schedule on Day 6 with iteration.collect (which has the run count) or Day 9 with the orchestrator wiring. Acceptance: 'inspect output includes a Cost section with low/high range and a one-line recommendation.'

9) THE FAILURE-MODE ACCEPTANCE CRITERION (most important). An agent that forgets threads exist will silently lump everything into one giant pile and the killer demo dies. Add to Day 10 dogfood acceptance: 'verify that during dogfood the [thread] prefix appeared on the stdout of every executors run / orchestrators run invocation, that thread show @active was called by the agent before iteration_video, and that no run was attributed to thread @active when a more recent thread had been opened (lineage inference worked).' Make it observable, not assumed.

10) DOCUMENT WHEN EACH TIER FIRES in docs/threads.md so agents reading the doc can predict what they'll see. Day 5 deliverable, scoped to one short table.

These items are load-bearing for adoption: if the agent UX is sloppy, the data the iteration video reads is sloppy, and the demo lies. They are not deferrable to a follow-up sprint.
- FINAL TIGHTENING NOTE before finalize. Eight surgical edits to plan v3. Each is a concrete plan-text change, not a directional nudge. Land all eight in v4.

1) DAY 3 OVERPACK + DOC TIMING (single fix). Day 3 is now larger than v1 (auto-attribute + prefix + tiered Notice/Warn + 5 CLI subcommands + lazy reaper + --no-content). Rebalance:
  - Move 'archive' and 'reopen' subcommands AND the lazy reaper from Day 3 -> Day 4 morning.
  - Move the SKILL.md paragraph, the 'executors inspect' / 'orchestrators inspect' footer, and a one-page docs/threads.md#what-the-prefix-means STUB (with the tier-fires table) from Day 5 -> Day 3, so agents on Day 3 see the [thread] prefix AND the docs that explain it on the same day.
  - Day 5 keeps docs/threads.md long-form reference (Privacy, Concurrent Selection, Deferred Features), brief snapshot, provenance, backfill.

2) LINE-ORDERING RULE. Add to Day 2 deliverables + acceptance: 'All prefix lines are written to stdout in this exact order before any other command output: [thread], then [variants] (when present), then any Notice line, then any Warn line, then a blank separator line, then command output.' Add tests/test_threads_prefix_ordering.py that synthesizes a run with all four lines firing and asserts the order.

3) FAILURE-MODE DOGFOOD ACCEPTANCE (most important). Add to Day 10 acceptance verbatim: 'During the dogfood render, capture stdout of every executors run / orchestrators run invocation. Assert observably: (a) every invocation emitted a [thread] prefix line on stdout BEFORE other output; (b) the prefix correctly identified the active thread by ULID; (c) lineage inference fired when any --brief / --asset / --video / --input arg pointed inside runs/<R>/; (d) the [variants] nag fired for the logo-candidate-producing run and was silenced after thread keep; (e) thread show @active was called by the operating agent before iteration_video. These are observable assertions written into the dogfood log, not subjective notes.'

4) EXACT SKILL.MD VERBATIM WORDING. Pin the sentence on Day 3, not Day 5. Plan must commit to landing this exact text (or a refined version via a one-line note in the plan): 'At the start of any session that will produce runs, run python3 -m artagents thread show @active first. The [thread] prefix on every command output is your continuous indicator; if it shows the wrong thread, run thread new or pass --thread @new to your next command. Selections are append-only; the most recent write is authoritative on read; prior selections are preserved as history but do not affect current keepers.' (Combines SKILL.md anchor + SD-042 last-write-wins in one paragraph the agent reads once and acts on.)

5) OUTPUT-FORMAT STABILITY in Definition of Done. Add clause (k): 'The prefix line format ([thread] / [variants] / Notice / Warn) is part of the public CLI contract. Format changes require a major schema_version bump and a transition period documented in docs/threads.md. The format is treated as load-bearing for agent compatibility.'

6) FROZEN-SCHEMA CHECKPOINT at end of Day 2. Add to Day 2 acceptance: 'schema_version=1 is frozen at end of Day 2. Any field addition / removal / rename to SD-008 fields after end-of-Day-2 requires shipping an SD-039 migration helper plus a .bak rotation test in the same PR. This checkpoint is enforced in PR review for the rest of the sprint.'

7) NESTED-EXECUTOR ENV THREADING explicit deliverable. Day 3 deliverables must name verbatim: 'Modify _run_external_executor at artagents/executors/runner.py:258 to thread ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID into the subprocess env when present, so external executor processes inherit the parent thread without re-stamping.' Add tests/test_threads_nested_env.py asserting parent->child inheritance and skip-begin behavior when env is set.

8) TIER DISCIPLINE acceptance criterion on Day 10. Add: 'In the dogfood run plus a synthetic 50-run replay, the WARN tier fires on <=5% of executor/orchestrator invocations. If above 5%, the brief-novelty trigger threshold is tuned before sign-off. Notice tier has no rate cap but is logged for review.'

These are the final items. After v4 lands them, plan goes to finalize. Items not in this note ([hint] line for fan-out, [thread-health] smell line, demo screen-share framing) are accepted as deferred to execution-time judgment.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Sprint Plan Document for Thread/Variant/Iteration-Video Layer

## Overview
Produce `docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`: a 2-week (10 working day) sprint plan that turns `docs/design-thread-layer.md`'s Settled Decisions (SD-001…SD-044) into day-by-day deliverables. The deliverable is a planning document, not code. SD-035 fixes the week split, SD-036 fixes scope cuts. SD-041 (privacy/redaction), SD-042 (concurrent variant-selection docs), and SD-043 (iteration-video cost guardrail) are load-bearing. SD-044 is non-load-bearing context.

Document sections required: one-page exec summary, day-by-day plan with per-day acceptance criteria, **Agent UX Surface** (load-bearing for adoption), critical path, risks + mitigations, integration test plan (rolling up SD-037), dogfood plan against `runs/artagents_logo_v3` (with 60-second demo milestone), and explicit resolutions to the six Open Questions.

Repo grounding (verified):
- Chokepoint: `artagents/executors/runner.py:75` (`run_executor`) and `artagents/orchestrators/runner.py`. **`run_executor` short-circuits `upload.youtube` at `runner.py:78`**; this special path must be exercised by the no-op test plan (SD-030 wrapper never runs for it).
- `_run_external_executor` builds subprocess env at `runner.py:258` — Day-3 nested-thread env propagation lives here.
- `ExecutorRunRequest` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:43-54`) and `OrchestratorRunRequest` have **no** `thread` field today; CLI plumbing is an explicit Day-2 prerequisite.
- Variant-producing tools: `artagents/executors/generate_image/run.py`, `artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py`.
- Fixture: `runs/artagents_logo_v3/` with `concepts.json`, `grid.jpg`, `logo-manifest.json`, `logo-plan.json`, `prompts.json`, `images/logo-00N.png`.
- `requirements.txt` declares filelock/jsonschema/openai/anthropic/etc. but NOT `xxhash`; no `pyproject.toml`. Day 1 must add `xxhash>=3.4` to `requirements.txt`.
- `tests/` is pytest-based; existing coverage must remain green (SD-034, SD-037).
- `SKILL.md` exists at repo root.

Approach assessment vs. flags: none of the open flags suggest a wrong target. All are additive content the user explicitly mandated in two new note blocks (ADDITIONAL GAPS 1-8 and AGENT UX LAYER GAPS 1-10). Plan structure stands; this revision lands all 18 items as discrete day-tagged plan entries.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Author the exec summary (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Open** with `# Sprint Plan: Thread / Variant / Iteration-Video Layer`.
2. **Write** a one-page `## Executive Summary` (~45 lines) covering: goal, why-now (SD-008 irreversibility), scope-in (SD-035 + SD-041/042/043), scope-out (SD-036 verbatim), critical path one-liner, top 3 risks with concrete mitigations, definition-of-done. Include one sentence each on SD-041 redaction, SD-042 last-write-wins, SD-043 cost guardrail, the Agent UX surface, and the Day-10 60-second demo milestone.

### Step 2: Author Hard Constraints recap (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Hard Constraints (from design)` mapping each load-bearing SD (SD-001…SD-043) to the day it lands. Group by week.
2. **Add** the fail-closed reminder: default `role: ancillary`; only `generate_image` and `logo_ideas` opt in to `variant` per SD-031.

### Step 3: Author Open Question resolutions (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Open Question Resolutions`:
   - **OQ-1** day allocation → see Week 1/2 sections below.
   - **OQ-2** summarize parallelism → `concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=int(os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_CONCURRENCY", "4")))` with exponential backoff per `builtin.understand` call.
   - **OQ-3** prefix line format → width `min(120, $COLUMNS)`, ANSI color iff `sys.stdout.isatty()` and `$NO_COLOR` unset, label truncate to 32 chars with `…`, never truncate IDs. **Stream:** stdout for all prefix lines. **Order:** `[thread]` → `[variants]` → `[hint]` → `Notice:` → `Warn:` → blank line → command output. **Stability:** the prefix-line format is governed by `schema_version`; format changes require a major version bump and a transition period — added to Definition of Done.
   - **OQ-4** reaper timing → lazy on first index read per process.
   - **OQ-5** docs path → `docs/threads.md` (matches SD-038).
   - **OQ-6** quality_floor formula → `data_quality = 0.5 * parent_capture_score + 0.3 * fraction_with_brief_content_sha256 + 0.2 * fraction_with_at_least_one_input_artifact_resolved`, where `parent_capture_score = (runs_with_parents + valid_roots) / total_runs` and a run is a `valid_root` iff `input_artifacts == []`. Floor 0.6 per SD-024. Refusal report names only unresolved-producer runs (never valid roots).

### Step 4: Author Week 1 — Data Layer days 1–5 (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** `## Week 1: Data Layer (Days 1–5)` with `### Day N` subsections (Goal / Deliverables / SDs landed / Acceptance / Tests):

   - **Day 1 — Index foundation + IDs + dependency glue.** Deliverables: `artagents/threads/{ids,index,schema}.py` with `fcntl.flock` (30s acquire timeout; on timeout error message points at `thread gc --force-unlock`) + tmp+fsync+`os.replace` + `.bak` rotate (SD-003), Crockford ULIDs (SD-004), `schema_version: 1` with N-1 reader (SD-039), repo-relative paths only (SD-040). **Append `xxhash>=3.4` to `requirements.txt`** (no pyproject.toml). Acceptance: 8 parallel writes preserve all updates; corrupted file recovers from `.bak`; `pip install -r requirements.txt` resolves cleanly and `import xxhash` succeeds; lock-acquire timeout error message includes `thread gc --force-unlock`. Tests: `tests/test_threads_index.py`, `tests/test_threads_dependencies.py`.

   - **Day 2 — `run.json` + chokepoint wrapper + CLI/request plumbing + redaction + line-ordering + Frozen Schema Review.** Deliverables:
     - `artagents/threads/{record,wrapper}.py` writing all SD-008 fields.
     - **Modify `ExecutorRunRequest` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:43`) and `OrchestratorRunRequest` to add `thread: str | None = None`, `variants: int | None = None`, `from_variant: str | None = None`.**

The `secret`-prefixed env-var convention from SD-041 (Day 5) extends this with the env-var path. `runs/<slug>/private/` is treated as opaque (sha-only `input_artifacts` entries with `kind: "private"`).
     - **Line-ordering rule** (Definition of Done item): every prefix block prints to stdout in fixed order — `[thread]` → `[variants]` → `[hint]` → `Notice:` lines → `Warn:` lines → exactly one blank line → command output.

   - **Day 3 — Auto-attribute + prefix lines + nested env propagation + thread show/new/list + agent docs (rebalanced; SD-006/SD-018/SD-038 priority).** Deliverables:
     - `artagents/threads/{attribute,prefix}.py`: 5-branch decision per SD-006 (lineage inference scans args for `runs/<R>/` paths) under lock; prefix lines per SD-018 + OQ-3 (line-ordering, color, truncation rules).
     - **Tier firing rules implemented** (the policy itself is documented in the Agent UX Surface section): **Info** = every run; **Notice** = first-run-of-process, gap >1h since last thread run, cwd change, auto-reopen of archived thread; **Warn** = brief novelty (Levenshtein distance vs prior thread brief above a threshold). Warn must fire <5% of runs in dogfood (Day-10 acceptance check).
     - `python3 -m artagents thread {new, list, show}` (3 of 5 lifecycle subcommands; **`archive` and `reopen` move to Day 4 morning** to relieve Day 3). `thread show @active` accepts `--no-content` per SD-041.
     - **`thread list` emits a `[thread-health]` smell line** when ≥1 of: any thread has variant groups with 0 kept, ≥3 open threads idle >5d (detection only per SD-036; no auto-fix).
     - **`ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID` env propagation in `_run_external_executor` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:258`):** the wrapper sets `ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID=<thread_id>` in the child subprocess env; child wrappers skip `threads.begin` if the env var is set and join the parent thread instead. Tests: `tests/test_threads_nested.py` (parent → child inheritance via subprocess).
     - **SKILL.md paragraph appended (Day 3, not Day 5) per SD-038**, with exact verbatim text:
       > "At the start of any session that will produce runs, run `python3 -m artagents thread show @active` first. The `[thread]` prefix on every command output is your continuous indicator; if it shows the wrong thread, run `thread new` or pass `--thread @new`. Selections are append-only; the most recent write is authoritative on read; prior selections are preserved as history but do not affect current keepers (SD-042). Before any iteration-video render, always run `iteration_video inspect <thread>` first — it shows detected modalities, chosen renderers, estimated cost, and quality score so you can adjust before committing to a render."
     - **Footer line in `executors inspect` and `orchestrators inspect` output (Day 3) naming the active thread** so agents seeing prefix lines on Day 3 already have explanatory docs.
     - **Risk-day cut-list:** if Day 3 slips past 6pm, in priority order DEFER (a) `[thread-health]` smell line, then (b) `Warn:` brief-novelty trigger (keep Info/Notice; ship Warn Day 4), then (c) `thread list` (keep `thread show`/`thread new`). Auto-attribute, prefix lines, nested env, and SKILL.md paragraph + inspect footer are NOT cut.
     - Acceptance: prefix line printed BEFORE rest of stdout in OQ-3 order; `[thread-health]` smell line fires when seeded; child subprocess inherits `ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID`; SKILL.md paragraph contains the exact verbatim text above; `executors inspect` / `orchestrators inspect` footer names active thread. Tests: `tests/test_threads_attribute.py` (5 branches with frozen clock), `tests/test_threads_prefix.py` (line ordering + tier firing), `tests/test_threads_nested.py`, `tests/test_threads_health_smell.py`, `tests/test_threads_skill_md_text.py` (asserts exact verbatim string in SKILL.md).

Soft nudge, not warning. Schedule on Day 4 with the `[variants]` nag.

Tests: `tests/test_threads_variants.py`, `tests/test_threads_variants_help.py`, `tests/test_threads_hint.py`, `tests/test_threads_reaper.py`.

### Step 5: Author Week 2 — Iteration Video MVP days 6–10 (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** `## Week 2: Iteration Video MVP (Days 6–10)`:

   - **Day 6 — Modality registry + `iteration.collect`.** Deliverables: `artagents/modalities/__init__.py` (registry per SD-025), `artagents/modalities/{image_grid,audio_waveform,generic_card}.py` declaring `kinds`/`clip_modes`/`default_clip_mode_for(shape, style)`/`produces_audio`/`cost_hint`; `python3 -m artagents modalities {list, inspect}`; `artagents/executors/iteration_collect/` walks `parent_run_ids` backward (provenance-graph default per SD-023); computes `data_quality` per the corrected OQ-6 formula. Acceptance: `iteration.quality.json` lists missing signals and distinguishes valid roots from unresolved-producer runs; only 3 renderers register; `generic_card` is always last in fallback chain.

   - **Day 7 — `iteration.summarize` (SD-043 cap inside the executor) + `iteration.score` + summary cache.** Deliverables:
     - `artagents/executors/iteration_summarize/` calls `builtin.understand` via `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_CONCURRENCY|4)` with per-call exponential backoff.
     - **SD-043 cap enforced INSIDE this executor:** before any `builtin.understand` dispatch, count input runs; if `count > max_iterations` (default 200, configurable via `--max-iterations` and `ARTAGENTS_ITERATION_MAX` env), refuse with non-zero exit and an actionable message naming the cap, the override flag, and the env var. **Direct `executors run iteration.summarize` cannot bypass.**
     - **SD-043 cache** at `.artagents/iteration_cache/<run_id>__<summarizer_model_version>.json`; cap evaluates against uncached count.
     - `artagents/executors/iteration_score/` — deterministic ordering by causal depth + selection-event tiebreaks.
     - Acceptance: 12 parallel summarize calls finish in <4× single-call wallclock; `executors run iteration.summarize` on a 250-iteration set refuses BEFORE any `builtin.understand` dispatch; re-running on a thread that grew by 3 runs only summarizes the 3 new runs (cache hits visible in stdout); score deterministic. Tests: `tests/test_iteration_summarize_cap.py` (executor-level via CLI), `tests/test_iteration_summarize_cache.py`, `tests/test_iteration_score.py`.

   - **Day 8 — `iteration.assemble` + quality floor.** Deliverables: `(kind, preview_modes)` → renderer per SD-026; precedence `theme > direction > style > defaults` per SD-027; audio-bed selection per SD-028 (>40% audio coverage → iterations-as-bed; never generative music); emit `iteration.timeline.json`. Quality floor refusal per SD-024: `data_quality < 0.6` prints actionable `thread …` backfill commands per unresolved-producer run; `--force` bypass logged in provenance. Acceptance: refusal report never names valid roots; bypassed render carries `forced: true`. Tests: `tests/test_iteration_assemble.py`, `tests/test_quality_floor.py` (incl. root-heavy thread does NOT refuse).

   - **Day 9 — `builtin.iteration_video` orchestrator + report + loud fallback + Cost section in inspect.** Deliverables: `artagents/orchestrators/iteration_video/` chains `iteration.collect → iteration.summarize → iteration.score → iteration.assemble → builtin.cut → builtin.render` per SD-021; emits SD-022 5-tuple as a single variant group; 5 flags + 1 inspect command per SD-027 (`--renderers`, `--clip-mode`, `--direction` label-only per SD-036, `--mode chaptered`, `--audio-bed`); `--max-iterations` is forwarded into `iteration.summarize`.
     - **`iteration_video inspect <thread>` Cost section (SD-043 UX preview):** prints a `## Cost` block with low/high range computed as `N_uncached_runs * (per_call_cost_low, per_call_cost_high)` for the configured summarizer plus a one-line recommendation (`OK to render`, `costly: consider --max-iterations N`, or `refused at executor: above cap`); also prints detected modalities, chosen renderers, defaults, and a `Quality:` line.
     - SD-029 loud fallback: `generic_card` use produces an `<aside class="renderer-fallback">no renderer for kind:&lt;X&gt;</aside>` annotation in `iteration.report.html` AND a stdout `Warn:` line.
     - `iteration.report.html` honors `--no-content` per SD-041.
     - Acceptance: `inspect runs/artagents_logo_v3-thread` lists `image` + `image_grid` + a Cost block with low/high range; injecting `kind: model_3d` triggers both warnings; on a 250-iteration synthetic thread `inspect` warns AND orchestrator dispatch is refused at the Day-7 executor layer. Tests: `tests/test_iteration_video_inspect.py` (Cost block low/high + recommendation), `tests/test_iteration_video_no_content.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_fallback.py`.

   - **Day 10 — Dogfood, demo milestone, failure-mode acceptance.** Deliverables: end-to-end render of `runs/artagents_logo_v3` (after Day 5 backfill adopts it) producing all 5 SD-022 outputs with `data_quality ≥ 0.6`; full SD-037 integration suite green.
     - **60-second demo milestone:** produce a 60-second `iteration.mp4` against `runs/artagents_logo_v3`, screen-share it live, and check off a manual checklist that includes: video plays end-to-end; `iteration.report.html` opens; "in-thread" vs "pulled-by-ancestry" labels present; `chosen_from_groups` reflects the kept variant; Cost block visible in `inspect`; `--no-content` mode renders sha-only; loud-fallback path verified by injection.
     - **Failure-mode acceptance (observable):** during the dogfood session, capture stdout from every `executors run` and `orchestrators run` invocation and verify (a) the `[thread]` prefix appeared on each one; (b) `python3 -m artagents thread show @active` was invoked by the agent before any `iteration_video` call; (c) lineage inference attributed every run correctly (no run was attributed to `@active` when a more recent thread had been opened). Captured in `tests/test_dogfood_failure_modes.py` as a transcript-replay test against a recorded dogfood log.
     - **Warn-tier discipline check:** count `Warn:` lines emitted across the dogfood session; assert <5% of total runs emitted a `Warn:` (else trigger thresholds get tuned before sign-off).
     - Acceptance: all of the above green; full `pytest` green; SD-037 tests all green; demo manually signed off with date and reviewer name.

### Step 6: Author Agent UX Surface (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `## Agent UX Surface` after Week 2, with explicit day-tagged commitments (matches AGENT UX LAYER GAPS 1–10):
   - **UX-1 Tier discipline (Day 3 implementation, Day 5 documentation):** Info every run; Notice on first-run-of-process / gap >1h / cwd change / auto-reopen; Warn on brief novelty (rare). Day-10 acceptance: Warn fires <5% of runs in dogfood or thresholds tuned before sign-off.
   - **UX-2 `[hint]` line (Day 4):** 3+ sequential same-executor runs without `--variants` → `[hint]` suggesting `thread group <ids>`. Soft nudge, not warning.
   - **UX-3 `[thread-health]` smell line (Day 3):** on `thread list`, surface conditions like "12 variant groups, 0 kept" or "3 open threads idle >5d". Detection only.
   - **UX-4 SKILL.md exact wording (Day 3):** the verbatim paragraph in Day 3 deliverables is committed; refinements during sprint require explicit decision.
   - **UX-5 Output-format stability:** `[thread]`/`[variants]`/`[hint]`/Notice/Warn line formats are part of the public CLI contract, governed by `schema_version`. Format changes require a major version bump and a transition period. Added to Definition of Done.
   - **UX-6 Line-ordering rule (Day 2):** stdout, in fixed order `[thread]` → `[variants]` → `[hint]` → `Notice:` → `Warn:` → blank line → command output. `tests/test_threads_line_ordering.py` enforces.
   - **UX-7 Inspect-before-render (Day 3 SKILL.md + Day 5 docs/threads.md):** SKILL.md and docs/threads.md mandate running `iteration_video inspect <thread>` before any iteration-video render.
   - **UX-8 Cost section (Day 9):** `iteration_video inspect` prints a Cost block with low/high range and a one-line recommendation.
   - **UX-9 Failure-mode dogfood acceptance (Day 10):** observable verification that every executor/orchestrator run emitted a `[thread]` prefix, that `thread show @active` ran before iteration_video, and that lineage inference prevented misattribution.
   - **UX-10 Tier firing table (Day 5 in `docs/threads.md`):** one short table mapping tier → triggers → examples so agents can predict what they'll see.

### Step 7: Author Critical Path + Risks (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `## Critical Path`: Day-1 atomic write + xxhash → Day-2 wrapper + CLI plumbing + line-ordering + Frozen Schema Review → Day-3 auto-attribute + nested env + SKILL.md + inspect footer → Day-4 variants + lifecycle CLI tail + reaper → Day-5 brief+provenance+docs/threads.md → Day-6 collect → Day-7 summarize+cap+cache → Day-8 assemble+quality floor → Day-9 orchestrator+Cost section → Day-10 dogfood+60-second demo+failure-mode acceptance. Day 5 is the latest possible date for SD-008 metadata. End-of-Day-2 Frozen Schema Review is a critical milestone.
2. **Add** `## Risks and Mitigations` with concrete mitigations:
   - **Day-3 overpack risk:** auto-attribute + prefix + nested env + 3 lifecycle subcommands + SKILL.md + inspect footer is ~7-8h. **Mitigation:** Day-3 risk-day cut-list (defer `[thread-health]`, then Warn brief-novelty, then `thread list`) — see Day 3 deliverables. `archive`/`reopen`/reaper already moved to Day 4 morning.
   - **Lock contention** under SD-037 8-parallel test → fcntl 30s acquire timeout; on timeout the error message explicitly points at `python3 -m artagents thread gc --force-unlock`. Acceptance test: 8-parallel set with one stuck owner produces the actionable error.
   - **Chokepoint regression** breaking existing tests → wrapper no-op gates (SD-030); `upload.youtube` short-circuit specifically tested (`tests/test_threads_upload_youtube_noop.py`); full pytest gated each day.
   - **Fixture insufficiency** → Day-5 backfill adopts `runs/artagents_logo_v3`; if floor < 0.6, `--force` plus annotated report still demonstrates loud-fallback path.
   - **Schema bug post-Day-2** → Frozen Schema Review milestone gates every later schema change behind an SD-039 migration helper + `.bak` rotation test added BEFORE the change merges.
   - **`builtin.understand` rate limits** → `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_CONCURRENCY|4)` with exponential backoff per call; `ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_SEQUENTIAL=1` fallback; SD-043 cache reduces N.
   - **Privacy regression (SD-041)** → path-based opt-in only (`runs/<slug>/private/`); `--no-content` on `thread show` and `iteration.report.html`; broadened `cli_args_redacted` (KEY/TOKEN/SECRET/[REDACTED]); redaction tests gate Day 2.
   - **Cost blowup (SD-043)** → cap inside `iteration.summarize`; `inspect` Cost section previews low/high before render.
   - **Dependency drift** → Day-1 adds `xxhash>=3.4` to `requirements.txt`; `tests/test_threads_dependencies.py` asserts importable.
   - **Warn spam** → Day-10 acceptance requires Warn <5% of dogfood runs; if exceeded, thresholds tuned before sign-off.
   - **Scope creep into SD-036 cuts** → any deferred-feature ask logged to `docs/threads.md#deferred`.

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1–2 (exec summary + constraints) first.
2. Step 3 (OQ resolutions, including the corrected OQ-6 and OQ-3 line-ordering) before day-by-day.
3. Step 4 (Week 1) before Step 5 (Week 2).
4. Step 6 (Agent UX Surface) — aggregates day commitments into one section for agent reviewers.
5. Step 7 (critical path + risks) after both weeks scoped.
6. Step 8 (integration + dogfood) — aggregates concrete file paths.
7. Step 9 (closing) last.

## Validation Order
1. Confirm every load-bearing SD (SD-001…SD-043) appears in some day's "SDs landed" line or in scope/cuts.
2. Confirm SD-041 introduces NO `--brief-private` flag.
3. Confirm SD-043 cap is enforced INSIDE `iteration.summarize` on Day 7.
4. Confirm Day 1 schedules adding `xxhash>=3.4` to `requirements.txt`.

    "OQ-6 corrected: data_quality = 0.5*parent_capture_score + 0.3*has_brief_sha + 0.2*has_resolved_input_artifact, where parent_capture_score = (runs_with_parents + valid_roots)/total_runs and valid_root iff input_artifacts==[]; floor 0.6.",
    "Three modality renderers per SD-035: image_grid, audio_waveform, generic_card.",
    "Dogfood fixture is runs/artagents_logo_v3 with verified contents.",
    "Day 5 is the latest possible day for shipping SD-008 metadata; hard gate.",
    "End-of-Day-2 Frozen Schema Review is a hard milestone gating all later schema work.",
    "The sprint plan deliverable is purely planning content; no code is written by the executor for this deliverable.",
    "IDLE_ARCHIVE_WINDOW=7d, IDLE_REOPEN_WINDOW=48h.",
    "Existing pytest suite must remain green without modification (SD-034).",
    "schema_version starts at 1 for all SD-039 records.",
    "SD-041 brief privacy is opt-in solely via the `runs/<slug>/private/` path convention; no new user-facing flag is added.",
    "SD-008 cli_args_redacted regex is case-insensitive `(KEY|TOKEN|SECRET|PASSWORD|PASSPHRASE|API_?KEY|BEARER)`; matched argv values become `***REDACTED***`.",
    "SD-043 max_iterations default is 200; configurable via --max-iterations flag and ARTAGENTS_ITERATION_MAX env; enforcement lives inside the iteration.summarize executor.",
    "SD-043 summary cache key is (run_id, summarizer_model_version) under .artagents/iteration_cache/.",
    "SD-044 is non-load-bearing context recorded in Out-of-Scope/closing sections.",
    "xxhash version pin is xxhash>=3.4; requirements.txt is the canonical dependency declaration since this repo has no pyproject.toml.",
    "ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID is the env var children read in _run_external_executor (runner.py:258); when present, child wrapper skips begin and inherits parent thread.",
    "Day-3 risk-day cut-list priority order: defer [thread-health] smell line, then Warn brief-novelty trigger, then thread list; auto-attribute/prefix/nested-env/SKILL.md/inspect-footer are NEVER cut.",
    "Tier firing rules (committed): Info every run; Notice on first-run-of-process / gap >1h / cwd change / auto-reopen of archived thread; Warn on brief-novelty (rare).",
    "Warn-tier discipline target: <5% of runs in dogfood emit a Warn line; failure triggers threshold tuning before sign-off.",
    "Day-10 demo target: 60-second iteration.mp4 against runs/artagents_logo_v3, screen-shared, manual checklist signed off with date and reviewer name.",
    "Failure-mode dogfood checks are observable and recorded: stdout transcript replayed by tests/test_dogfood_failure_modes.py."
  ],

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

This is critique iteration 4. The template file includes prior findings with their status. Verify addressed flags were actually fixed, re-flag if inadequate, and check for new issues introduced by the revision.

        Additional guidelines:
        - Robustness level: standard. Use balanced judgment. Flag significant risks, but do not spend flags on minor polish or executor-obvious boilerplate.
        - Over-engineering: prefer the simplest approach that fully solves the problem.
        - Reuse existing flag IDs when the same concern is still open.
        - `verified_flag_ids`: list flag IDs from prior iterations that the revised plan actually resolves (e.g., if the plan was revised to fix FLAG-001, and you confirm the fix is correct, include "FLAG-001"). Only include flags you've verified — don't guess.
        - Verify that the plan follows the expected structure when validator warnings or the outline suggest drift.
        - Additional flags may use these categories: correctness, security, completeness, performance, maintainability, other.
        - Focus on concrete issues, not structural formatting.
